{"type":"tool_use","timestamp":1787455181941,"sessionID":"ses_fd35ea0e5ffe34F6Skn5PFxymm","part":{"type":"tool","tool":"read","callID":"call_01a02ca19299736282d59beab36b5a2e","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"filePath":"/workspaces/powerchoice/src/app_shell.py"},"output":"<path>/workspaces/powerchoice/src/app_shell.py</path>\n<type>file</type>\n<content>\n1: \"\"\"PowerChoice application shell — the bridge between pvf_app_runner and the application.\n2: \n3: Named in pvf_app_startup.yaml (application.shell_module / application.entry_point).\n4: The entry point receives the PvfInvocation, wires application settings, models, hooks,\n5: and routers into it, and returns None on success (or an error message on failure).\n6: \"\"\"\n7: from .pvf.bindings.pvf_invocation import PvfAppTarget, PvfInvocation\n8: \n9: _OPENAPI_TAGS_SESSION = [\n10:     {\n11:         \"name\": \"powerchoice_session_api\",\n12:         \"description\": \"Session Based web service operations for **PowerChoice Service** - UI focused API for single page application and specialized applications.\",\n13:         \"externalDocs\": {\n14:             \"description\": \"See Also: Toolkit Request API Documentation Endpoint\",\n15:             \"url\": \"{base_url}/api/docs\",\n16:         },\n17:     },\n18:     {\"name\": \"manage-projects\", \"description\": \"Services to manage customer defined projects\"},\n19:     {\"name\": \"manage-project-content\", \"description\": \"Services to manage project alternatives, factors/criteria, and factor templates\"},\n20:     {\"name\": \"api-keys\", \"description\": \"Services to manage customer assigned API keys bound to applications\"},\n21:     {\"name\": \"dashboard\", \"description\": \"Services to access the general dashboard purposes\"},\n22:     {\"name\": \"share\", \"description\": \"Authenticated services to create and manage project share links\"},\n23:     {\"name\": \"project-votes\", \"description\": \"Logged-in voting sessions, observations, and project report analytics\"},\n24:     {\"name\": \"user-communication\", \"description\": \"User Communication — submit and review bug reports and suggestions\"},\n25:     {\"name\": \"deprecated\", \"description\": \"Calls that are pending deprecation - see notes for alternatives\"},\n26:     {\"name\": \"test-helpers\", \"description\": \"Testing only. Endpoints used by automated and e2e tests; not registered in production.\"},\n27: ]\n28: \n29: _OPENAPI_TAGS_SHARE = [\n30:     {\"name\": \"share_ext\", \"description\": \"External (cookie/magic-key) access to shared project vote, status, and report views\"},\n31: ]\n32: \n33: _OPENAPI_TAGS_EXTERNAL = [\n34:     {\n35:         \"name\": \"powerchoice_toolkit_api\",\n36:         \"description\": \"Toolkit Request API access to PowerChoice Service\",\n37:         \"externalDocs\": {\n38:             \"description\": \"See Also: Session Based API Documentation Endpoint\",\n39:             \"url\": \"{base_url}/session/docs\",\n40:         },\n41:     },\n42:     {\"name\": \"general\", \"description\": \"Retrieve defined projects and other toolkit details\"},\n43:     {\"name\": \"callback_examples\", \"description\": \"Examples of callback payload configurations\"},\n44: ]\n45: \n46: \n47: def register_app_hooks(hooks) -> None:\n48:     \"\"\"Register PowerChoice hooks on a PvfHookRegistry.\n49: \n50:     Called by app_startup (server process) and by the watcher process, which runs\n51:     separately and needs the same application bindings (webhook payload builders, etc.).\n52:     \"\"\"\n53:     from .utils.base_classes_and_enums import OutboundEmailType\n54:     from .db.models.customer_projects import CustomerProject\n55:     from .db.models.project_vote_events import ProjectVoteParticipant\n56:     from .pvf.bindings.pvf_invocation import PvfResolvedEntity\n57: \n58:     def powerchoice_entity_resolver(session, usr_context, entity_id: int) -> PvfResolvedEntity | None:\n59:         \"\"\"Resolve a CustomerProject for pvf share/branding validation, tenancy, and naming.\"\"\"\n60:         customer_id = usr_context.sess_user.customer_id if usr_context and usr_context.sess_user else None\n61:         if customer_id is None:\n62:             return None\n63:         project = CustomerProject.get_customer_project_by_id_system(session=session, customer_id=customer_id, id=entity_id, clear_lock=False)\n64:         if project is None:\n65:             return None\n66:         return PvfResolvedEntity(display_name=project.project_title or project.project_tag, customer_id=project.customer_id)\n67: \n68:     _SHARE_EMAIL_TYPES = {\n69:         \"vote\": OutboundEmailType.share_project_vote,\n70:         \"vote_view\": OutboundEmailType.share_project_vote_view,\n71:         \"report\": OutboundEmailType.share_project_report,\n72:     }\n73: \n74:     def share_activity_enricher(session, usr_context, shares: list) -> dict[int, dict]:\n75:         \"\"\"Participant/observation counts per share for the activity endpoints.\"\"\"\n76:         from sqlmodel import select\n77:         from sqlalchemy import func\n78: \n79:         stats: dict[int, dict] = {}\n80:         share_ids = [share.id for share in shares]\n81:         if not share_ids:\n82:             return stats\n83:         for share_id in share_ids:\n84:             stats[int(share_id)] = {\"participant_count\": 0, \"observation_count\": 0}\n85:         vote_rows = session.exec(\n86:             select(\n87:                 ProjectVoteParticipant.share_id,\n88:                 func.count(ProjectVoteParticipant.id),\n89:                 func.coalesce(func.sum(ProjectVoteParticipant.comparison_count), 0),\n90:             )\n91:             .where(\n92:                 ProjectVoteParticipant.customer_id == usr_context.sess_user.customer_id,\n93:                 ProjectVoteParticipant.share_id.in_(share_ids),\n94:                 ProjectVoteParticipant.deleted_date.is_(None),\n95:             )\n96:             .group_by(ProjectVoteParticipant.share_id)\n97:         ).all()\n98:         for share_id, part_count, obs_sum in vote_rows:\n99:             if share_id is not None:\n100:                 stats[int(share_id)] = {\"participant_count\": int(part_count or 0), \"observation_count\": int(obs_sum or 0)}\n101:         return stats\n102: \n103:     hooks.entity_resolver = powerchoice_entity_resolver\n104:     hooks.share_type_email_dispatcher = _SHARE_EMAIL_TYPES.get\n105:     hooks.share_resend_email_dispatcher = lambda _share_type: OutboundEmailType.resending_invitation\n106:     hooks.share_action_matrix = {\n107:         share_type: [\"vote\", \"view\", \"report\"]\n108:         for share_type in (\"vote\", \"vote_view\", \"report\")\n109:     }\n110:     hooks.share_mutating_operations = [\"vote\"]\n111:     hooks.share_activity_enricher = share_activity_enricher\n112: \n113: \n114: def app_startup(invocation: PvfInvocation) -> str | None:\n115:     \"\"\"PowerChoice startup entry point called by pvf_app_runner with the PvfInvocation.\"\"\"\n116:     # 1. application settings (subclass of PvfGlobalSettings) + startup value merge\n117:     from .config.config_settings import settings\n118:     from .config.config_client import ClientSettings, client_settings\n119:     from .pvf.bindings.pvf_startup_config import merge_pvf_settings_into\n120: \n121:     merge_pvf_settings_into(settings)\n122:     invocation.app_settings = settings\n123:     invocation.app_client_settings = client_settings\n124:     invocation.client_session_context_settings_type = ClientSettings\n125:     invocation.application_name = settings.APPLICATION_NAME\n126:     invocation.application_version = settings.VERSION\n127:     \n128:     # 2. register application table metadata (also the alembic probe models_module)\n129:     from .db.models import bootstrap  # noqa: F401\n130: \n131:     # 3. application startup tasks: seed system shared templates; sample data outside prod\n132:     from .pvf.bindings.pvf_services import get_next_session, log_event, populate_test_customer_and_user\n133:     from .utils.bootstrap_system_templates import bootstrap_system_shared_templates\n134: \n135:     try:\n136:         with get_next_session() as session:\n137:             bootstrap_system_shared_templates(session)\n138:     except Exception as ex:\n139:         log_event(\n140:             f\"app_startup: bootstrap_system_shared_templates failed: {ex}\",\n141:             severity=2,\n142:             ex_info=ex,\n143:         )\n144: \n145:     if settings.is_prod() is False and settings.BOOTSTRAP_SAMPLE_DATA:\n146: total 184 lines)\n</content>","metadata":{"preview":"\"\"\"PowerChoice application shell — the bridge between pvf_app_runner and the application.\n\nNamed in pvf_app_startup.yaml (application.shell_module / application.entry_point).\nThe entry point receives the PvfInvocation, wires application settings, models, hooks,\nand routers into it, and returns None on success (or an error message on failure).\n\"\"\"\nfrom .pvf.bindings.pvf_invocation import PvfAppTarget, PvfInvocation\n\n_OPENAPI_TAGS_SESSION = [\n    {\n        \"name\": \"powerchoice_session_api\",\n        \"description\": \"Session Based web service operations for **PowerChoice Service** - UI focused API for single page application and specialized applications.\",\n        \"externalDocs\": {\n            \"description\": \"See Also: Toolkit Request API Documentation Endpoint\",\n            \"url\": \"{base_url}/api/docs\",\n        },\n    },\n    {\"name\": \"manage-projects\", \"description\": \"Services to manage customer defined projects\"},\n    {\"name\": \"manage-project-content\", \"description\": \"Services to manage project alternatives, factors/criteria, and factor templates\"},\n    {\"name\": \"api-keys\", \"description\": \"Services to manage customer assigned API keys bound to applications\"},","truncated":false,"loaded":[],"display":{"type":"file","path":"/workspaces/powerchoice/src/app_shell.py","text":"\"\"\"PowerChoice application shell — the bridge between pvf_app_runner and the application.\n\nNamed in pvf_app_startup.yaml (application.shell_module / application.entry_point).\nThe entry point receives the PvfInvocation, wires application settings, models, hooks,\nand routers into it, and returns None on success (or an error message on failure).\n\"\"\"\nfrom .pvf.bindings.pvf_invocation import PvfAppTarget, PvfInvocation\n\n_OPENAPI_TAGS_SESSION = [\n    {\n        \"name\": \"powerchoice_session_api\",\n        \"description\": \"Session Based web service operations for **PowerChoice Service** - UI focused API for single page application and specialized applications.\",\n        \"externalDocs\": {\n            \"description\": \"See Also: Toolkit Request API Documentation Endpoint\",\n            \"url\": \"{base_url}/api/docs\",\n        },\n    },\n    {\"name\": \"manage-projects\", \"description\": \"Services to manage customer defined projects\"},\n    {\"name\": \"manage-project-content\", \"description\": \"Services to manage project alternatives, factors/criteria, and factor templates\"},\n    {\"name\": \"api-keys\", \"description\": \"Services to manage customer assigned API keys bound to applications\"},\n    {\"name\": \"dashboard\", \"description\": \"Services to access the general dashboard purposes\"},\n    {\"name\": \"share\", \"description\": \"Authenticated services to create and manage project share links\"},\n    {\"name\": \"project-votes\", \"description\": \"Logged-in voting sessions, observations, and project report analytics\"},\n    {\"name\": \"user-communication\", \"description\": \"User Communication — submit and review bug reports and suggestions\"},\n    {\"name\": \"deprecated\", \"description\": \"Calls that are pending deprecation - see notes for alternatives\"},\n    {\"name\": \"test-helpers\", \"description\": \"Testing only. Endpoints used by automated and e2e tests; not registered in production.\"},\n]\n\n_OPENAPI_TAGS_SHARE = [\n    {\"name\": \"share_ext\", \"description\": \"External (cookie/magic-key) access to shared project vote, status, and report views\"},\n]\n\n_OPENAPI_TAGS_EXTERNAL = [\n    {\n        \"name\": \"powerchoice_toolkit_api\",\n        \"description\": \"Toolkit Request API access to PowerChoice Service\",\n        \"externalDocs\": {\n            \"description\": \"See Also: Session Based API Documentation Endpoint\",\n            \"url\": \"{base_url}/session/docs\",\n        },\n    },\n    {\"name\": \"general\", \"description\": \"Retrieve defined projects and other toolkit details\"},\n    {\"name\": \"callback_examples\", \"description\": \"Examples of callback payload configurations\"},\n]\n\n\ndef register_app_hooks(hooks) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Register PowerChoice hooks on a PvfHookRegistry.\n\n    Called by app_startup (server process) and by the watcher process, which runs\n    separately and needs the same application bindings (webhook payload builders, etc.).\n    \"\"\"\n    from .utils.base_classes_and_enums import OutboundEmailType\n    from .db.models.customer_projects import CustomerProject\n    from .db.models.project_vote_events import ProjectVoteParticipant\n    from .pvf.bindings.pvf_invocation import PvfResolvedEntity\n\n    def powerchoice_entity_resolver(session, usr_context, entity_id: int) -> PvfResolvedEntity | None:\n        \"\"\"Resolve a CustomerProject for pvf share/branding validation, tenancy, and naming.\"\"\"\n        customer_id = usr_context.sess_user.customer_id if usr_context and usr_context.sess_user else None\n        if customer_id is None:\n            return None\n        project = CustomerProject.get_customer_project_by_id_system(session=session, customer_id=customer_id, id=entity_id, clear_lock=False)\n        if project is None:\n            return None\n        return PvfResolvedEntity(display_name=project.project_title or project.project_tag, customer_id=project.customer_id)\n\n    _SHARE_EMAIL_TYPES = {\n        \"vote\": OutboundEmailType.share_project_vote,\n        \"vote_view\": OutboundEmailType.share_project_vote_view,\n        \"report\": OutboundEmailType.share_project_report,\n    }\n\n    def share_activity_enricher(session, usr_context, shares: list) -> dict[int, dict]:\n        \"\"\"Participant/observation counts per share for the activity endpoints.\"\"\"\n        from sqlmodel import select\n        from sqlalchemy import func\n\n        stats: dict[int, dict] = {}\n        share_ids = [share.id for share in shares]\n        if not share_ids:\n            return stats\n        for share_id in share_ids:\n            stats[int(share_id)] = {\"participant_count\": 0, \"observation_count\": 0}\n        vote_rows = session.exec(\n            select(\n                ProjectVoteParticipant.share_id,\n                func.count(ProjectVoteParticipant.id),\n                func.coalesce(func.sum(ProjectVoteParticipant.comparison_count), 0),\n            )\n            .where(\n                ProjectVoteParticipant.customer_id == usr_context.sess_user.customer_id,\n                ProjectVoteParticipant.share_id.in_(share_ids),\n                ProjectVoteParticipant.deleted_date.is_(None),\n            )\n            .group_by(ProjectVoteParticipant.share_id)\n        ).all()\n        for share_id, part_count, obs_sum in vote_rows:\n            if share_id is not None:\n                stats[int(share_id)] = {\"participant_count\": int(part_count or 0), \"observation_count\": int(obs_sum or 0)}\n        return stats\n\n    hooks.entity_resolver = powerchoice_entity_resolver\n    hooks.share_type_email_dispatcher = _SHARE_EMAIL_TYPES.get\n    hooks.share_resend_email_dispatcher = lambda _share_type: OutboundEmailType.resending_invitation\n    hooks.share_action_matrix = {\n        share_type: [\"vote\", \"view\", \"report\"]\n        for share_type in (\"vote\", \"vote_view\", \"report\")\n    }\n    hooks.share_mutating_operations = [\"vote\"]\n    hooks.share_activity_enricher = share_activity_enricher\n\n\ndef app_startup(invocation: PvfInvocation) -> str | None:\n    \"\"\"PowerChoice startup entry point called by pvf_app_runner with the PvfInvocation.\"\"\"\n    # 1. application settings (subclass of PvfGlobalSettings) + startup value merge\n    from .config.config_settings import settings\n    from .config.config_client import ClientSettings, client_settings\n    from .pvf.bindings.pvf_startup_config import merge_pvf_settings_into\n\n    merge_pvf_settings_into(settings)\n    invocation.app_settings = settings\n    invocation.app_client_settings = client_settings\n    invocation.client_session_context_settings_type = ClientSettings\n    invocation.application_name = settings.APPLICATION_NAME\n    invocation.application_version = settings.VERSION\n    \n    # 2. register application table metadata (also the alembic probe models_module)\n    from .db.models import bootstrap  # noqa: F401\n\n    # 3. application startup tasks: seed system shared templates; sample data outside prod\n    from .pvf.bindings.pvf_services import get_next_session, log_event, populate_test_customer_and_user\n    from .utils.bootstrap_system_templates import bootstrap_system_shared_templates\n\n    try:\n        with get_next_session() as session:\n            bootstrap_system_shared_templates(session)\n    except Exception as ex:\n        log_event(\n            f\"app_startup: bootstrap_system_shared_templates failed: {ex}\",\n            severity=2,\n            ex_info=ex,\n        )\n\n    if settings.is_prod() is False and settings.BOOTSTRAP_SAMPLE_DATA:\n        populate_test_customer_and_user(get_next_session())\n\n    # 4. application hooks binding pvf internals to PowerChoice logic\n    register_app_hooks(invocation.hooks)\n\n    # 5. application routers targeted at the appropriate FastAPI apps\n    from .api.app_customer_project_management import router as app_customer_project_router\n    from .api.app_customer_project_content_management import router as app_customer_project_content_router\n    from .api.app_dashboard_views import router as app_entries_views_router\n    from .api.app_shared_link_ext_access import router as app_shared_link_ext_access_router\n    from .api.app_project_vote_events import router as app_project_vote_events_router\n    from .api.api_probe_customer_status import router as api_probe_services_router\n\n    invocation.add_router(PvfAppTarget.session, app_customer_project_router)\n    invocation.add_router(PvfAppTarget.session, app_customer_project_content_router)\n    invocation.add_router(PvfAppTarget.session, app_entries_views_router)\n    invocation.add_router(PvfAppTarget.session, app_project_vote_events_router)\n    invocation.add_router(PvfAppTarget.share, app_shared_link_ext_access_router)\n    invocation.add_router(PvfAppTarget.external, api_probe_services_router)\n\n    # Testing only. Endpoints used by automated and e2e tests; not registered in production.\n    if settings.is_prod() is False:\n        from .api.test_helpers import router as test_helpers_router\n\n        invocation.add_router(PvfAppTarget.session, test_helpers_router)\n\n    # 6. application OpenAPI tag metadata (merged by the runner)\n    def _with_base_url(tags: list[dict]) -> list[dict]:\n        return [\n            {**tag, \"externalDocs\": {**tag[\"externalDocs\"], \"url\": tag[\"externalDocs\"][\"url\"].format(base_url=settings.APPLICATION_BASE_URL)}}\n            if \"externalDocs\" in tag else tag\n            for tag in tags\n        ]\n\n    invocation.openapi_tags[PvfAppTarget.session].extend(_with_base_url(_OPENAPI_TAGS_SESSION))\n    invocation.openapi_tags[PvfAppTarget.share].extend(_with_base_url(_OPENAPI_TAGS_SHARE))\n    invocation.openapi_tags[PvfAppTarget.external].extend(_with_base_url(_OPENAPI_TAGS_EXTERNAL))\n\n    return None","lineStart":1,"lineEnd":184,"totalLines":184,"truncated":false}},"title":"src/app_shell.py","time":{"start":1787455181729,"end":1787455181925}},"metadata":{"openai":{"itemId":"fc_01a02ca19299736282d59beab36b5a2e"}},"id":"prt_02ca19306001tlO5sCx3hm5GO8","sessionID":"ses_fd35ea0e5ffe34F6Skn5PFxymm","messageID":"msg_02ca18c4c001Q5epq0jG3BUKzk"}}

\"\"\"pvf_app_runner — the standard, executable application runner for pvf-based applications.\n2: \n3: Startup orchestration:\n4:   1. load pvf_app_startup.yaml (bindings, feature switches, share vocabularies)\n5:   2. ingest the configured env file (usually .env) with python-dotenv\n6:   3. inject startup-owned values into pvf_settings (read-only thereafter)\n7:   4. establish the database connection FIRST; on failure, clear diagnostics to stderr\n8:      and exit with an error code\n9:   5. register pvf model metadata (feature-gated)\n10:   6. build the four FastAPI applications (noauth / session / share / external) and the\n11:      root app; register pvf-owned routers\n12:   7. invoke the application shell entry point with a PvfInvocation; it registers hooks\n13:      and routers and returns None (success), an error message, or raises\n14:   8. complete setup: include application routers, merge OpenAPI tag metadata, run the\n15:      dependency-injection integrity check, install handlers, mount apps and static files\n16: \n17: Run with:  fastapi dev src/pvf/pvf_app_runner.py   (or fastapi run ... for production)\n18: The module-level `app` is the composed ASGI root application.\n19: \"\"\"\n20: import importlib\n21: import json\n22: import os\n23: import re\n24: import sys\n25: from pathlib import Path\n26: from traceback import format_exc\n27: \n28: from fastapi import FastAPI\n29: from fastapi.exceptions import RequestValidationError\n30: from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware\n31: from fastapi.openapi.docs import get_swagger_ui_html\n32: from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse\n33: from fastapi.staticfiles import StaticFiles\n34: from sqlalchemy import text\n35: \n36:     \n37: from .bindings.pvf_startup_config import (\n38:     PvfStartupConfig,\n39:     PvfStartupConfigError,\n40:     apply_startup_config,\n41:     load_startup_config,\n42: )\n43: \n44: _WORKSPACE_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]  # pvf -> src -> workspace root\n45: _STATIC_DIR = str(_WORKSPACE_ROOT / \"static\")\n46: _STATIC_CLIENT_DIR = str(_WORKSPACE_ROOT / \"static_client\")\n47: \n48: # Mount prefixes for the four FastAPI applications\n49: MOUNT_PREFIXES = {\"noauth\": \"/auth-ws\", \"session\": \"/ws\", \"share\": \"/ext-ws\", \"external\": \"/api\"}\n50: \n51: # Non-app paths (reserved prefixes) always return JSON 404s rather than the SPA page\n52: RESERVED_PREFIXES = [\"/ws\", \"/ext-ws\", \"/api\", \"/auth\", \"/hook-stripe-events\", \"/api/webhooks\"]\n53: \n54: EXIT_BAD_STARTUP_CONFIG = 2\n55: EXIT_DATABASE_FAILED = 3\n56: EXIT_APP_STARTUP_FAILED = 4\n57: \n58: # The composed applications, populated by build_app(); consumed by tooling\n59: # (OpenAPI snapshot extraction, diagnostics).\n60: composed_apps: dict[str, FastAPI] = {}\n61: \n62: _PVF_TAGS = {\n63:     \"noauth\": [\n64:         {\"name\": \"auth\", \"description\": \"Services to support authentication\"},\n65:         {\"name\": \"self-reg\", \"description\": \"Self Registration Services for Initial Signup\"},\n66:     ],\n67:     \"session\": [\n68:         {\"name\": \"admin\", \"description\": \"Services to customer/user administration and customer/user information\"},\n69:         {\"name\": \"context\", \"description\": \"Services to access the user/customer context and related user information\"},\n70:         {\"name\": \"devops\", \"description\": \"Services to access and post log events for diagnostic purposes\"},\n71:     ],\n72:     \"share\": [],\n73:     \"external\": [\n74:         {\"name\": \"access_check\", \"description\": \"Signed API connectivity checks\"},\n75:     ],\n76: }\n77: \n78: \n79: def _fatal(message: str, exit_code: int) -> None:\n80:     print(f\"FATAL: {message}\", file=sys.stderr)\n81:     raise SystemExit(exit_code)\n82: \n83: \n84: def _load_env_file(startup_config: PvfStartupConfig) -> None:\n85:     \"\"\"Ingest the configured env file (usually .env). Process environment wins.\"\"\"\n86:     from dotenv import load_dotenv\n87: \n88:     env_path = Path(startup_config.env_file)\n89:     if not env_path.is_absolute():\n90:         env_path = _WORKSPACE_ROOT / env_path\n91:     if env_path.is_file():\n92:         load_dotenv(env_path, override=False)\n93: \n94: \n95: def _probe_database_or_exit(db, dsn: str) -> None:\n96:     try:\n97:         with db.get_engine().connect() as connection:\n98:             connection.execute(text(\"SELECT 1\"))\n99:     except Exception as ex:\n100:         masked_dsn = re.sub(r\"://([^:@/]+):([^@/]+)@\", r\"://\\1:****@\", dsn)\n101:         _fatal(\n102:             \"could not establish a connection to the database.\\n\"\n103:             f\"  DSN: {masked_dsn}\\n\"\n104:             f\"  error: {type(ex).__name__}: {ex}\",\n105:             EXIT_DATABASE_FAILED,\n106:         )\n107: \n108: \n109: def _merge_openapi_specs(title: str, specs: list[tuple[str, FastAPI]]) -> dict:\n110:     \"\"\"Merge (mount_prefix, app) OpenAPI specs into one schema; paths are prefixed.\n111: \n112:     Component name collisions are tolerated only when the schemas are identical\n113:     (shared pvf models); conflicting definitions are a build error.\n114:     \"\"\"\n115:     merged: dict | None = None\n116:     for prefix, sub_app in specs:\n117:         spec = sub_app.openapi()\n118:         if merged is None:\n119:             merged = {\"openapi\": spec[\"openapi\"], \"info\": {\"title\": title, \"version\": spec[\"info\"].get(\"version\", \"\")},\n120:                       \"paths\": {}, \"components\": {\"schemas\": {}}, \"tags\": []}\n121:         for path, item in spec.get(\"paths\", {}).items():\n122:             merged[\"paths\"][prefix + path] = item\n123:         for name, schema in spec.get(\"components\", {}).get(\"schemas\", {}).items():\n124:             existing = merged[\"components\"][\"schemas\"].get(name)\n125:             if existing is not None and existing != schema:\n126:                 raise RuntimeError(f\"Conflicting OpenAPI schema definitions for '{name}' across composed apps\")\n127:             merged[\"components\"][\"schemas\"][name] = schema\n128:         seen_tags = {t[\"name\"] for t in merged[\"tags\"]}\n129:         for tag in spec.get(\"tags\", []):\n130:             if tag[\"name\"] not in seen_tags:\n131:                 merged[\"tags\"].append(tag)\n132:                 seen_tags.add(tag[\"name\"])\n133:     return merged or {}\n134: \n135: \n136: _sessions_openapi_cache: dict = {}\n137: \n138: \n139: def build_sessions_openapi() -> dict:\n140:     \"\"\"The aggregate session-facing contract: root + noauth + session + share apps.\"\"\"\n141:     if not _sessions_openapi_cache:\n142:         specs = [(\"\", composed_apps[\"root\"])]\n143:         for target in (\"noauth\", \"session\", \"share\"):\n144:             specs.append((MOUNT_PREFIXES[target], composed_apps[target]))\n145:         _sessions_openapi_cache.update(_merge_openapi_specs(\"Session UI API\", specs))\n146:     return _sessions_openapi_cache\n147: \n148: \n149: def build_external_openapi() -> dict:\n150:     return composed_apps[\"external\"].openapi()\n151: \n152: \n153: def build_app(config_path: str | None = None) -> FastAPI:\n154:     global composed_apps\n155: \n156:     # 1. startup YAML\n157:     try:\n158:         startup_config = load_startup_config(config_path)\n159:     except PvfStartupConfigError as ex:\n160:         _fatal(str(ex), EXIT_BAD_STARTUP_CONFIG)\n161: \n162:     # 2. env file (python-dotenv; process environment overrides file values)\n163:     _load_env_file(startup_config)\n164: \n165:     # 3. settings + read-only startup value injection\n166:     from .config.pvf_config_settings import pvf_settings\n167: \n168:     apply_startup_config(startup_config, pvf_settings)\n169:     pvf_settings.process_name = os.path.basename(__file__)\n170: \n171:     if not pvf_settings.UNIQUE_CONFIGURATION_CHECK:\n172:         _fatal(\n173:             \"This system appears to be running on an example or missing configuration file; \"\n174:             \"check the .env contents (UNIQUE_CONFIGURATION_CHECK must be 1).\",\n175:             EXIT_BAD_STARTUP_CONFIG,\n176:         )\n177: \n178:     # 4. database connection first\n179:     from .db.connect import DatabaseConnection\n180: \n181:     db = DatabaseConnection()\n182:     _probe_database_or_exit(db, pvf_settings.DB_PATH_OR_CONNECTION_STRING)\n183: \n184:     # 4b. application schema extensions must be declared before any model import:\n185:     # import the shell module (declarations only; no models at module level by\n186:     # contract) and let it register added columns on pvf tables\n187:     try:\n188:         shell_module = importlib.import_module(startup_config.application.shell_module)\n189:     except Exception as ex:\n190:         _fatal(\n191:             f\"could not import application shell module '{startup_config.application.shell_module}': {ex}\",\n192:             EXIT_APP_STARTUP_FAILED,\n193:         )\n194:     from .db.model_factory import get_schema_extension_registry\n195: \n196:     register_schema_extensions = getattr(shell_module, \"register_schema_extensions\", None)\n197:     if callable(register_schema_extensions):\n198:         register_schema_extensions(get_schema_extension_registry())\n199: \n200:     # 5. pvf model metadata (feature-gated)\n201:     from .db.models import pvf_bootstrap\n202: \n203:     pvf_bootstrap.import_core_models()\n204:     pvf_bootstrap.import_feature_models(\n205:         activate_stripe_integration=pvf_settings.ACTIVATE_STRIPE_INTEGRATION,\n206:         activate_branding_image_store=pvf_settings.ACTIVATE_BRANDING_IMAGE_STORE,\n207:         activate_share_links=pvf_settings.ACTIVATE_SHARE_LINKS,\n208:         activate_external_api=pvf_settings.ACTIVATE_EXTERNAL_API,\n209:         activate_customer_communication=pvf_settings.ACTIVATE_CUSTOMER_COMMUNICATION,\n210:     )\n211: \n212:     # 6. build the (bindings, feature switches, share vocabularies)\n  2. ingest the configured env file (usually .env) with python-dotenv\n  3. inject startup-owned values into pvf_settings (read-only thereafter)\n  4. establish the database connection FIRST; on failure, clear diagnostics to stderr\n     and exit with an error code\n  5. register pvf model metadata (feature-gated)\n  6. build the four FastAPI applications (noauth / session / share / external) and the\n     root app; register pvf-owned routers\n  7. invoke the application shell entry point with a PvfInvocation; it registers hooks\n     and routers and returns None (success), an error message, or raises\n  8. complete setup: include application routers, merge OpenAPI tag metadata, run the\n     dependency-injection integrity check, install handlers, mount apps and static files\n\nRun with:  fastapi dev src/pvf/pvf_app_runner.py   (or fastapi run ... for production)\nThe module-level `app` is the composed ASGI root executable application runner for pvf-based applications.\n\nStartup orchestration:\n  1. load pvf_app_startup.yaml (bindings, feature switches, share vocabularies)\n  2. ingest the configured env file (usually .env) with python-dotenv\n  3. inject startup-owned values into pvf_settings (read-only thereafter)\n  4. establish the database connection FIRST; on failure, clear diagnostics to stderr\n     and exit with an error code\n  5. register pvf model metadata (feature-gated)\n  6. build the four FastAPI applications (noauth / session / share / external) and the\n     root app; register pvf-owned routers\n  7. invoke the application shell entry point with a PvfInvocation; it registers hooks\n     and routers and returns None (success), an error message, or raises\n  8. complete setup: include application routers, merge OpenAPI tag metadata, run the\n     dependency-injection integrity check, install handlers, mount apps and static files\n\nRun with:  fastapi dev src/pvf/pvf_app_runner.py   (or fastapi run ... for production)\nThe module-level `app` is the composed ASGI root application.\n\"\"\"\nimport importlib\nimport json\nimport os\nimport re\nimport sys\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom traceback import format_exc\n\nfrom fastapi import FastAPI\nfrom fastapi.exceptions import RequestValidationError\nfrom fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware\nfrom fastapi.openapi.docs import get_swagger_ui_html\nfrom fastapi.responses import JSONResponse\nfrom fastapi.staticfiles import StaticFiles\nfrom sqlalchemy import text\n\n    \nfrom .bindings.pvf_startup_config import (\n    PvfStartupConfig,\n    PvfStartupConfigError,\n    apply_startup_config,\n    load_startup_config,\n)\n\n_WORKSPACE_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]  # pvf -> src -> workspace root\n_STATIC_DIR = str(_WORKSPACE_ROOT / \"static\")\n_STATIC_CLIENT_DIR = str(_WORKSPACE_ROOT / \"static_client\")\n\n# Mount prefixes for the four FastAPI applications\nMOUNT_PREFIXES = {\"noauth\": \"/auth-ws\", \"session\": \"/ws\", \"share\": \"/ext-ws\", \"external\": \"/api\"}\n\n# Non-app paths (reserved prefixes) always return JSON 404s rather than the SPA page\nRESERVED_PREFIXES = [\"/ws\", \"/ext-ws\", \"/api\", \"/auth\", \"/hook-stripe-events\", \"/api/webhooks\"]\n\nEXIT_BAD_STARTUP_CONFIG = 2\nEXIT_DATABASE_FAILED = 3\nEXIT_APP_STARTUP_FAILED = 4\n\n# The composed applications, populated by build_app(); consumed by tooling\n# (OpenAPI snapshot extraction, diagnostics).\ncomposed_apps: dict[str, FastAPI] = {}\n\n_PVF_TAGS = {\n    \"noauth\": [\n        {\"name\": \"auth\", \"description\": \"Services to support authentication\"},\n        {\"name\": \"self-reg\", \"description\": \"Self Registration Services for Initial Signup\"},\n    ],\n    \"session\": [\n        {\"name\": \"admin\", \"description\": \"Services to customer/user administration and customer/user information\"},\n        {\"name\": \"context\", \"description\": \"Services to access the user/customer context and related user information\"},\n        {\"name\": \"devops\", \"description\": \"Services to access and post log events for diagnostic purposes\"},\n    ],\n    \"share\": [],\n    \"external\": [\n        {\"name\": \"access_check\", \"description\": \"Signed API connectivity checks\"},\n    ],\n}\n\n\ndef _fatal(message: str, exit_code: int) -> None:\n    print(f\"FATAL: {message}\", file=sys.stderr)\n    raise SystemExit(exit_code)\n\n\ndef _load_env_file(startup_config: PvfStartupConfig) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Ingest the configured env file (usually .env). Process environment wins.\"\"\"\n    from dotenv import load_dotenv\n\n    env_path = Path(startup_config.env_file)\n    if not env_path.is_absolute():\n        env_path = _WORKSPACE_ROOT / env_path\n    if env_path.is_file():\n        load_dotenv(env_path, override=False)\n\n\ndef _probe_database_or_exit(db, dsn: str) -> None:\n    try:\n        with db.get_engine().connect() as connection:\n            connection.execute(text(\"SELECT 1\"))\n    except Exception as ex:\n        masked_dsn = re.sub(r\"://([^:@/]+):([^@/]+)@\", r\"://\\1:****@\", dsn)\n        _fatal(\n            \"could not establish a connection to the database.\\n\"\n            f\"  DSN: {masked_dsn}\\n\"\n            f\"  error: {type(ex).__name__}: {ex}\",\n            EXIT_DATABASE_FAILED,\n        )\n\n\ndef _merge_openapi_specs(title: str, specs: list[tuple[str, FastAPI]]) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Merge (mount_prefix, app) OpenAPI specs into one schema; paths are prefixed.\n\n    Component name collisions are tolerated only when the schemas are identical\n    (shared pvf models); conflicting definitions are a build error.\n    \"\"\"\n    merged: dict | None = None\n    for prefix, sub_app in specs:\n        spec = sub_app.openapi()\n        if merged is None:\n            merged = {\"openapi\": spec[\"openapi\"], \"info\": {\"title\": title, \"version\": spec[\"info\"].get(\"version\", \"\")},\n                      \"paths\": {}, \"components\": {\"schemas\": {}}, \"tags\": []}\n        for path, item in spec.get(\"paths\", {}).items():\n            merged[\"paths\"][prefix + path] = item\n        for name, schema in spec.get(\"components\", {}).get(\"schemas\", {}).items():\n            existing = merged[\"components\"][\"schemas\"].get(name)\n            if existing is not None and existing != schema:\n                raise RuntimeError(f\"Conflicting OpenAPI schema definitions for '{name}' across composed apps\")\n            merged[\"components\"][\"schemas\"][name] = schema\n        seen_tags = {t[\"name\"] for t in merged[\"tags\"]}\n        for tag in spec.get(\"tags\", []):\n            if tag[\"name\"] not in seen_tags:\n                merged[\"tags\"].append(tag)\n                seen_tags.add(tag[\"name\"])\n    return merged or {}\n\n\n_sessions_openapi_cache: dict = {}\n\n\ndef build_sessions_openapi() -> dict:\n    \"\"\"The aggregate session-facing contract: root + noauth + session + share apps.\"\"\"\n    if not _sessions_openapi_cache:\n        specs = [(\"\", composed_apps[\"root\"])]\n        for target in (\"noauth\", \"session\", \"share\"):\n            specs.append((MOUNT_PREFIXES[target], composed_apps[target]))\n        _sessions_openapi_cache.update(_merge_openapi_specs(\"Session UI API\", specs))\n    return _sessions_openapi_cache\n\n\ndef build_external_openapi() -> dict:\n    return composed_apps[\"external\"].openapi()\n\n\ndef build_app(config_path: str | None = None) -> FastAPI:\n    global composed_apps\n\n    # 1. startup YAML\n    try:\n        startup_config = load_startup_config(config_path)\n    except PvfStartupConfigError as ex:\n        _fatal(str(ex), EXIT_BAD_STARTUP_CONFIG)\n\n    # 2. env file (python-dotenv; process environment overrides file values)\n    _load_env_file(startup_config)\n\n    # 3. settings + read-only startup value injection\n    from .config.pvf_config_settings import pvf_settings\n\n    apply_startup_config(startup_config, pvf_settings)\n    pvf_settings.process_name = os.path.basename(__file__)\n\n    if not pvf_settings.UNIQUE_CONFIGURATION_CHECK:\n        _fatal(\n            \"This system appears to be running on an example or missing configuration file; \"\n            \"check the .env contents (UNIQUE_CONFIGURATION_CHECK must be 1).\",\n            EXIT_BAD_STARTUP_CONFIG,\n        )\n\n    # 4. database connection first\n    from .db.connect import DatabaseConnection\n\n    db = DatabaseConnection()\n    _probe_database_or_exit(db, pvf_settings.DB_PATH_OR_CONNECTION_STRING)\n\n    # 4b. application schema extensions must be declared before any model import:\n    # import the shell module (declarations only; no models at module level by\n    # contract) and let it register added columns on pvf tables\n    try:\n        shell_module = importlib.import_module(startup_config.application.shell_module)\n    except Exception as ex:\n        _fatal(\n            f\"could not import application shell module '{startup_config.application.shell_module}': {ex}\",\n            EXIT_APP_STARTUP_FAILED,\n        )\n    from .db.model_factory import get_schema_extension_registry\n\n    register_schema_extensions = getattr(shell_module, \"register_schema_extensions\", None)\n    if callable(register_schema_extensions):\n        register_schema_extensions(get_schema_extension_registry())\n\n    # 5. pvf model metadata (feature-gated)\n    from .db.models import pvf_bootstrap\n\n    pvf_bootstrap.import_core_models()\n    pvf_bootstrap.import_feature_models(\n        activate_stripe_integration=pvf_settings.ACTIVATE_STRIPE_INTEGRATION,\n        activate_branding_image_store=pvf_settings.ACTIVATE_BRANDING_IMAGE_STORE,\n        activate_share_links=pvf_settings.ACTIVATE_SHARE_LINKS,\n        activate_external_api=pvf_settings.ACTIVATE_EXTERNAL_API,\n        activate_customer_communication=pvf_settings.ACTIVATE_CUSTOMER_COMMUNICATION,\n    )\n\n    # 6. build the FastAPI applications\n    from .bindings.pvf_invocation import PvfAppTarget, PvfInvocation, set_current_invocation\n    from .utils.fastapi_app_handlers import (\n        handle_500_internal_error,\n        handle_422_unprocessable_entity,\n        make_spa_fallback_404_handler,\n    )\n\n    # per-app 404 defaults to JSON; the root app gets the SPA fallback below\n    sub_app_exceptions: dict = {}\n    if pvf_settings.PYTEST_ACTIVE is False:\n        # don't handle 500 errors while testing, so complete error message is visible in pytest results\n        sub_app_exceptions[500] = handle_500_internal_error\n\n    def _make_sub_app(title: str, target: str) -> FastAPI:\n        sub = FastAPI(title=title, openapi_tags=list(_PVF_TAGS[target]), exception_handlers=dict(sub_app_exceptions))\n\n        @sub.exception_handler(RequestValidationError)\n        async def _handle_422(request, exc):\n            return await handle_422_unprocessable_entity(request, exc)\n\n        return sub\n\n    app_noauth = _make_sub_app(f\"{pvf_settings.APPLICATION_NAME} Entry API\", \"noauth\")\n    app_session = _make_sub_app(f\"{pvf_settings.APPLICATION_NAME} Session UI API\", \"session\")\n    app_share = _make_sub_app(f\"{pvf_settings.APPLICATION_NAME} Share Link API\", \"share\")\n    app_external = _make_sub_app(f\"{pvf_settings.APPLICATION_NAME} Customer API\", \"external\")\n\n    for browser_app in (app_noauth, app_session, app_share):\n        browser_app.add_middleware(\n            CORSMiddleware,\n            allow_origins=[\"http://localhost:3000\", pvf_settings.APPLICATION_BASE_URL],\n            allow_credentials=True,\n            allow_methods=[\"*\"],\n            allow_headers=[\"*\"],\n            expose_headers=[\"content-disposition\"],\n        )\n\n    # public liveness probe on the external app (exempt from the signed-header check)\n    from fastapi import APIRouter\n\n    external_minimal_router = APIRouter()\n\n    @external_minimal_router.get(\"/status\")\n    async def external_status():\n        return {\"service\": f\"{pvf_settings.APPLICATION_NAME} Service Customer Facing API\",\n                \"version\": pvf_settings.PVF_VERSION,\n                \"status\": \"API Available\"}\n\n    app_external.include_router(external_minimal_router)\n\n    # pvf-owned routers\n    from .api.auth import router as auth_router\n    from .api.log_events_request import router as log_events_request_router\n    from .api.user_requests import noauth_router as self_reg_router, router as user_requests_router\n\n    app_noauth.include_router(auth_router)\n    app_noauth.include_router(self_reg_router)\n    app_session.include_router(user_requests_router)\n    app_session.include_router(log_events_request_router)\n\n    root_exceptions = dict(sub_app_exceptions)\n    root_exceptions[404] = make_spa_fallback_404_handler(_STATIC_CLIENT_DIR, RESERVED_PREFIXES)\n    root_app = FastAPI(title=f\"{pvf_settings.APPLICATION_NAME} Service\", docs_url=\"/root-docs\", redoc_url=None,\n                       openapi_tags=[{\"name\": \"stripe-event-hook\", \"description\": \"Capture, log, and process updates from stripe via webhook\"}],\n                       exception_handlers=root_exceptions)\n\n    @root_app.exception_handler(RequestValidationError)\n    async def _root_handle_422(request, exc):\n        return await handle_422_unprocessable_entity(request, exc)\n\n    # SPA host routes (/alive, /)\n    from .api.hello_web import router as web_files_router\n\n    root_app.include_router(web_files_router)\n\n    # 7. application entry point (shell module imported above, before model registration)\n    invocation = PvfInvocation(\n        pvf_settings=pvf_settings,\n        startup_config=startup_config,\n        db=db,\n        app_noauth=app_noauth,\n        app_session=app_session,\n        app_share=app_share,\n        app_external=app_external,\n    )\n    set_current_invocation(invocation)\n\n    entry_point = getattr(shell_module, startup_config.application.entry_point, None)\n    if not callable(entry_point):\n        _fatal(\n            f\"application shell '{startup_config.application.shell_module}' has no callable \"\n            f\"'{startup_config.application.entry_point}'\",\n            EXIT_APP_STARTUP_FAILED,\n        )\n\n    try:\n        startup_result = entry_point(invocation)\n    except SystemExit:\n        raise\n    except Exception as ex:\n        _fatal(\n            f\"application startup raised {type(ex).__name__}: {ex}\\n{format_exc()}\",\n            EXIT_APP_STARTUP_FAILED,\n        )\n    if startup_result is not None:\n        _fatal(f\"application startup failed: {startup_result}\", EXIT_APP_STARTUP_FAILED)\n\n    from .api.app_context_views import (\n        bind_client_session_context_settings,\n        noauth_router as context_noauth_router,\n        router as context_router,\n    )\n    from .api.app_post_subscriber_transactions import router as subscriber_tx_router\n\n    if invocation.client_session_context_settings_type is not None:\n        bind_client_session_context_settings(invocation.client_session_context_settings_type)\n    app_noauth.include_router(context_noauth_router)\n    app_session.include_router(context_router)\n    app_session.include_router(subscriber_tx_router)\n\n    if pvf_settings.ACTIVATE_STRIPE_INTEGRATION:\n        from .api.hook_stripe_events import router as hook_stripe_events_router\n        root_app.include_router(hook_stripe_events_router)\n\n    if pvf_settings.ACTIVATE_BRANDING_IMAGE_STORE:\n        from .api.app_branding import router as branding_router, share_router as branding_share_router\n        app_session.include_router(branding_router)\n        app_share.include_router(branding_share_router)\n\n    if pvf_settings.ACTIVATE_SHARE_LINKS:\n        from .api.share_link_manage import router as share_link_manage_router\n        app_session.include_router(share_link_manage_router)\n\n    if pvf_settings.ACTIVATE_EXTERNAL_API:\n        from .api.api_access_management import router as api_access_management_router\n        from .api.api_probe import router as api_probe_router\n        app_session.include_router(api_access_management_router)\n        app_external.include_router(api_probe_router)\n\n    if pvf_settings.ACTIVATE_CUSTOMER_COMMUNICATION:\n        from .api.app_user_communication import router as user_communication_router\n        app_session.include_router(user_communication_router)\n        \n    # include application routers and merge tag metadata\n    for target in PvfAppTarget:\n        target_app = invocation.target_app(target)\n        for registration in invocation.routers[target]:\n            target_app.include_router(registration.router)\n        target_app.openapi_tags.extend(invocation.openapi_tags[target])\n\n    # 8. dependency-injection integrity (strict, regardless of endpoint parameters)\n    from .utils.dependency_integrity import verify_app_dependency_integrity\n\n    verify_app_dependency_integrity(app_noauth, \"noauth\")\n    verify_app_dependency_integrity(app_session, \"session\")\n    verify_app_dependency_integrity(app_share, \"share\")\n    verify_app_dependency_integrity(app_external, \"external\")\n\n    # root status (reports the application's identity/version when supplied by the shell)\n    @root_app.get(\"/status\")\n    async def root_status():\n        return {\"service\": f\"{invocation.application_name or pvf_settings.APPLICATION_NAME} Service UI API\",\n                \"version\": invocation.application_version or pvf_settings.PVF_VERSION,\n                \"status\": \"API Available\"}\n\n    # aggregate session-surface contract (root + noauth + session + share)\n    @root_app.get(\"/session/docs.json\", include_in_schema=False)\n    async def sessions_openapi():\n        return JSONResponse(build_sessions_openapi())\n\n    @root_app.get(\"/session/docs\", include_in_schema=False)\n    async def sessions_docs():\n        return get_swagger_ui_html(openapi_url=\"/session/docs.json\", title=\"Session UI API - Docs\")\n\n    # mounts: API prefixes first, static last\n    root_app.mount(MOUNT_PREFIXES[\"external\"], app_external, name=\"external_api\")\n    root_app.mount(MOUNT_PREFIXES[\"noauth\"], app_noauth, name=\"noauth_api\")\n    root_app.mount(MOUNT_PREFIXES[\"session\"], app_session, name=\"session_api\")\n    root_app.mount(MOUNT_PREFIXES[\"share\"], app_share, name=\"share_api\")\n\n    root_app.mount(\"/static\", StaticFiles(directory=_STATIC_DIR), name=\"static\")\n    root_app.mount(\"/\", StaticFiles(directory=_STATIC_CLIENT_DIR), name=\"static_client_files\")\n\n    composed_apps = {\n        \"root\": root_app,\n        \"noauth\": app_noauth,\n        \"session\": app_session,\n        \"share\": app_share,\n        \"external\": app_external,\n    }\n\n    from .utils.utils_show import show_vars_semi\n\n    show_vars_semi(\"pvf_app_runner is running\", app_name=pvf_settings.APPLICATION_NAME, pvf_vers=pvf_settings.PVF_VERSION, pid=os.getpid())\n    return root_app\n\n\napp = {"type":"tool_use","timestamp":1787455187117,"sessionID":"ses_fd35ea0e5ffe34F6Skn5PFxymm","part":{"type":"tool","tool":"read","callID":"call_01a02ca1a38d743082104a2792ff9c20","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"filePath":"/workspaces/powerchoice/src/pvf/bindings/pvf_invocation.py"},"output":"<path>/workspaces/powerchoice/src/pvf/bindings/pvf_invocation.py</path>\n<type>file</type>\n<content>\n1: \"\"\"PvfInvocation — the binding contract between pvf_app_runner and the application shell.\n2: \n3: The runner builds this object and passes it to the application entry point named in\n4: pvf_app_startup.yaml. The application is expected to:\n5:   1. create its own settings object (typically subclassing PvfGlobalSettings) and call\n6:      pvf.config.pvf_startup_config.merge_pvf_settings_into() to receive startup-owned values\n7:   2. import its model modules (registering table metadata)\n8:   3. register any hooks connecting framework internals to application logic\n9:   4. add its routers via add_router() targeted at the appropriate FastAPI app\n10:   5. optionally extend openapi_tags and set application_version\n11: The entry point returns None on success, or an error message string on failure; it may\n12: also raise descriptive exceptions to signal startup failure.\n13: \"\"\"\n14: from enum import StrEnum\n15: from typing import Any, Callable\n16: \n17: from fastapi import APIRouter, FastAPI\n18: from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field\n19: \n20: from ..config.pvf_config_settings import PvfGlobalSettings, pvf_settings\n21: from ..utils.pvf_base_internal_resources import PvfClientAuthSettings\n22: \n23: class PvfAppTarget(StrEnum):\n24:     \"\"\"The four FastAPI applications composed by pvf_app_runner.\"\"\"\n25:     noauth = \"noauth\"        # no authorization expected: login, password reset, self registration\n26:     session = \"session\"      # logged-in session (JWT cookie) endpoints\n27:     share = \"share\"          # share link access (cookie / magic-key gate, no session JWT)\n28:     external = \"external\"    # external API (signed headers)\n29: \n30: \n31: class RouterRegistration(BaseModel):\n32:     model_config = ConfigDict(arbitrary_types_allowed=True)\n33:     router: APIRouter\n34:     target: PvfAppTarget\n35: \n36: \n37: class PvfResolvedEntity(BaseModel):\n38:     \"\"\"Result of the application's entity_resolver hook.\"\"\"\n39:     display_name: str | None = None\n40:     customer_id: int | None = None\n41: \n42: \n43: class PvfHookRegistry(BaseModel):\n44:     \"\"\"Hooks connecting pvf internals to application-specific logic.\n45: \n46:     Applications fill these in during startup. All hooks are optional unless a feature\n47:     that needs them is activated in pvf_app_startup.yaml.\n48:     \"\"\"\n49:     model_config = ConfigDict(arbitrary_types_allowed=True)\n50: \n51:     # Entities: resolve an application entity (the thing a share or branding image\n52:     # references) for validation, tenancy checks, and display naming.\n53:     # Signature: (session, usr_context, entity_id) -> PvfResolvedEntity | None\n54:     entity_resolver: Callable | None = None\n55:     # Share links: map a share type (object action) to the outbound email type used for\n56:     # invitations, or None to skip. Signature: (share_type: str) -> str | Enum | None\n57:     share_type_email_dispatcher: Callable | None = None\n58:     # Share links: map a share type to the outbound email type used when a project\n59:     # administrator resends an existing invitation. Signature matches the dispatcher.\n60:     share_resend_email_dispatcher: Callable | None = None\n61:     # Share links: valid access operations per share type. {share_type: [operation, ...]}\n62:     share_action_matrix: dict[str, list[str]] = Field(default_factory=dict)\n63:     # Share links: operations that mutate state (require an established cookie session).\n64:     share_mutating_operations: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)\n65:     # Share links: enrich activity rows with application stats.\n66:     # Signature: (session, shares: list) -> dict[int, dict] mapping share id -> extra fields\n67:     share_activity_enricher: Callable | None = None\n68:     # Share links: add application fields to share email template params.\n69:     # Signature: (share_link, base_params: dict) -> dict\n70:     share_email_params_enricher: Callable | None = None\n71:     # External API: per webhook type, build the callback payload and decide readiness.\n72:     # payload_builder signature: (session, event) -> dict\n73:     # readiness_predicate signature: (session, event) -> bool\n74:     webhook_payload_builders: dict[str, Callable] = Field(default_factory=dict)\n75:     webhook_readiness_predicates: dict[str, Callable] = Field(default_factory=dict)\n76: \n77: \n78: from ..db.model_factory import PvfSchemaExtensionRegistry, get_schema_extension_registry\n79: \n80: \n81: class PvfInvocation(BaseModel):\n82:     model_config = ConfigDict(arbitrary_types_allowed=True)\n83:     pvf_settings: PvfGlobalSettings = None          # PvfGlobalSettings (active, startup values applied)\n84:     startup_config: Any = None        # PvfStartupConfig as loaded from pvf_app_startup.yaml\n85:     db: Any = None                    # DatabaseConnection (engine already verified)\n86:     app_noauth: FastAPI | None = None\n87:     app_session: FastAPI | None = None\n88:     app_share: FastAPI | None = None\n89:     app_external: FastAPI | None = None\n90:     app_settings: BaseModel | None = None        # live app settings; recommended to have pvf settings merged in\n91:     app_client_settings: BaseModel | None = None  # live default client-session settings instance\n92:     # Informational; surfaced by the root /status endpoint.\n93:     application_name: str | None = None\n94:     application_version: str | None = None\n95:     client_session_context_settings_type: type | None = None\n96: \n97:     routers: dict[PvfAppTarget, list[RouterRegistration]] = Field(\n98:         default_factory=lambda: {target: [] for target in PvfAppTarget}\n99:     )\n100:     openapi_tags: dict[PvfAppTarget, list[dict]] = Field(\n101:         default_factory=lambda: {target: [] for target in PvfAppTarget}\n102:     )\n103:     hooks: PvfHookRegistry = Field(default_factory=PvfHookRegistry)\n104:     schema_extensions: PvfSchemaExtensionRegistry = Field(default_factory=get_schema_extension_registry)\n105: \n106:     def add_router(self, target: PvfAppTarget, router: APIRouter) -> None:\n107:         \"\"\"Register an application router for inclusion in the given FastAPI app.\n108: \n109:         Dependency injection is strictly enforced at composition time by the runner's\n110:         integrity check, regardless of whether the dependency data is an input parameter\n111:         for a given endpoint; see pvf.utils.dependency_integrity.\n112:         \"\"\"\n113:         self.routers[PvfAppTarget(target)].append(RouterRegistration(router=router, target=PvfAppTarget(target)))\n114: \n115:     def target_app(self, target: PvfAppTarget) -> FastAPI:\n116:         return {\n117:             PvfAppTarget.noauth: self.app_noauth,\n118:             PvfAppTarget.session: self.app_session,\n119:             PvfAppTarget.share: self.app_share,\n120:             PvfAppTarget.external: self.app_external,\n121:         }[PvfAppTarget(target)]\n122: \n123: \n124: # The runner records the active invocation here so pvf internals (share gate, webhook\n125: # delivery, ...) can reach registered hooks at request time without importing the app.\n126: invocation_context: PvfInvocation = None\n127: \n128: def set_current_invocation(invocation: PvfInvocation | None) -> None:\n129:     global invocation_context\n130:     invocation_context = invocation\n131: \n132: \n133: def get_current_invocation() -> PvfInvocation | None:\n134:     return invocation_context\n135: \n136: \n137: def get_hooks() -> PvfHookRegistry:\n138:     invocation = invocation_context\n139:     return invocation.hooks if invocation is not None else PvfHookRegistry()\n140: \n141: \n142: def runtime_settings() -> BaseModel:\n143:     \"\"\"Active settings for pvf request paths: the app instance when bound, else pvf_settings.\"\"\"\n144:     invocation = invocation_context\n145:     if invocation is not None and invocation.app_settings is not None:\n146:         return invocation.app_settings\n147:     return pvf_settings\n148: \n\n(End of file - total 148 lines)\n</content>","metadata":{"preview":"\"\"\"PvfInvocation — the binding contract between pvf_app_runner and the application shell.\n\nThe runner builds this object and passes it to the application entry point named in\npvf_app_startup.yaml. The application is expected to:\n  1. create its own settings object (typically subclassing PvfGlobalSettings) and call\n     pvf.config.pvf_startup_config.merge_pvf_settings_into() to receive startup-owned values\n  2. import its model modules (registering table metadata)\n  3. register any hooks connecting framework internals to application logic\n  4. add its routers via add_router() targeted at the appropriate FastAPI app\n  5. optionally extend openapi_tags and set application_version\nThe entry point returns None on success, or an error message string on failure; it may\nalso raise descriptive exceptions to signal startup failure.\n\"\"\"\nfrom enum import StrEnum\nfrom typing import Any, Callable\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, FastAPI\nfrom pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field\n\nfrom ..config.pvf_config_settings import PvfGlobalSettings, pvf_settings","truncated":false,"loaded":[],"display":{"type":"file","path":"/workspaces/powerchoice/src/pvf/bindings/pvf_invocation.py","text":"\"\"\"PvfInvocation — the binding contract between pvf_app_runner and the application shell.\n\nThe runner builds this object and passes it to the application entry point named in\npvf_app_startup.yaml. The application is expected to:\n  1. create its own settings object (typically subclassing PvfGlobalSettings) and call\n     pvf.config.pvf_startup_config.merge_pvf_settings_into() to receive startup-owned values\n  2. import its model modules (registering table metadata)\n  3. register any hooks connecting framework internals to application logic\n  4. add its routers via add_router() targeted at the appropriate FastAPI app\n  5. optionally extend openapi_tags and set application_version\nThe entry point returns None on success, or an error message string on failure; it may\nalso raise descriptive exceptions to signal startup failure.\n\"\"\"\nfrom enum import StrEnum\nfrom typing import Any, Callable\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, FastAPI\nfrom pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field\n\nfrom ..config.pvf_config_settings import PvfGlobalSettings, pvf_settings\nfrom ..utils.pvf_base_internal_resources import PvfClientAuthSettings\n\nclass PvfAppTarget(StrEnum):\n    \"\"\"The four FastAPI applications composed by pvf_app_runner.\"\"\"\n    noauth = \"noauth\"        # no authorization expected: login, password reset, self registration\n    session = \"session\"      # logged-in session (JWT cookie) endpoints\n    share = \"share\"          # share link access (cookie / magic-key gate, no session JWT)\n    external = \"external\"    # external API (signed headers)\n\n\nclass RouterRegistration(BaseModel):\n    model_config = ConfigDict(arbitrary_types_allowed=True)\n    router: APIRouter\n    target: PvfAppTarget\n\n\nclass PvfResolvedEntity(BaseModel):\n    \"\"\"Result of the application's entity_resolver hook.\"\"\"\n    display_name: str | None = None\n    customer_id: int | None = None\n\n\nclass PvfHookRegistry(BaseModel):\n    \"\"\"Hooks connecting pvf internals to application-specific logic.\n\n    Applications fill these in during startup. All hooks are optional unless a feature\n    that needs them is activated in pvf_app_startup.yaml.\n    \"\"\"\n    model_config = ConfigDict(arbitrary_types_allowed=True)\n\n    # Entities: resolve an application entity (the thing a share or branding image\n    # references) for validation, tenancy checks, and display naming.\n    # Signature: (session, usr_context, entity_id) -> PvfResolvedEntity | None\n    entity_resolver: Callable | None = None\n    # Share links: map a share type (object action) to the outbound email type used for\n    # invitations, or None to skip. Signature: (share_type: str) -> str | Enum | None\n    share_type_email_dispatcher: Callable | None = None\n    # Share links: map a share type to the outbound email type used when a project\n    # administrator resends an existing invitation. Signature matches the dispatcher.\n    share_resend_email_dispatcher: Callable | None = None\n    # Share links: valid access operations per share type. {share_type: [operation, ...]}\n    share_action_matrix: dict[str, list[str]] = Field(default_factory=dict)\n    # Share links: operations that mutate state (require an established cookie session).\n    share_mutating_operations: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)\n    # Share links: enrich activity rows with application stats.\n    # Signature: (session, shares: list) -> dict[int, dict] mapping share id -> extra fields\n    share_activity_enricher: Callable | None = None\n    # Share links: add application fields to share email template params.\n    # Signature: (share_link, base_params: dict) -> dict\n    share_email_params_enricher: Callable | None = None\n    # External API: per webhook type, build the callback payload and decide readiness.\n    # payload_builder signature: (session, event) -> dict\n    # readiness_predicate signature: (session, event) -> bool\n    webhook_payload_builders: dict[str, Callable] = Field(default_factory=dict)\n    webhook_readiness_predicates: dict[str, Callable] = Field(default_factory=dict)\n\n\nfrom ..db.model_factory import PvfSchemaExtensionRegistry, get_schema_extension_registry\n\n\nclass PvfInvocation(BaseModel):\n    model_config = ConfigDict(arbitrary_types_allowed=True)\n    pvf_settings: PvfGlobalSettings = None          # PvfGlobalSettings (active, startup values applied)\n    startup_config: Any = None        # PvfStartupConfig as loaded from pvf_app_startup.yaml\n    db: Any = None                    # DatabaseConnection (engine already verified)\n    app_noauth: FastAPI | None = None\n    app_session: FastAPI | None = None\n    app_share: FastAPI | None = None\n    app_external: FastAPI | None = None\n    app_settings: BaseModel | None = None        # live app settings; recommended to have pvf settings merged in\n    app_client_settings: BaseModel | None = None  # live default client-session settings instance\n    # Informational; surfaced by the root /status endpoint.\n    application_name: str | None = None\n    application_version: str | None = None\n    client_session_context_settings_type: type | None = None\n\n    routers: dict[PvfAppTarget, list[RouterRegistration]] = Field(\n        default_factory=lambda: {target: [] for target in PvfAppTarget}\n    )\n    openapi_tags: dict[PvfAppTarget, list[dict]] = Field(\n        default_factory=lambda: {target: [] for target in PvfAppTarget}\n    )\n    hooks: PvfHookRegistry = Field(default_factory=PvfHookRegistry)\n    schema_extensions: PvfSchemaExtensionRegistry = Field(default_factory=get_schema_extension_registry)\n\n    def add_router(self, target: PvfAppTarget, router: APIRouter) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Register an application router for inclusion in the given FastAPI app.\n\n        Dependency injection is strictly enforced at composition time by the runner's\n        integrity check, regardless of whether the dependency data is an input parameter\n        for a given endpoint; see pvf.utils.dependency_integrity.\n        \"\"\"\n        self.routers[PvfAppTarget(target)].append(RouterRegistration(router=router, target=PvfAppTarget(target)))\n\n    def target_app(self, target: PvfAppTarget) -> FastAPI:\n        return {\n            PvfAppTarget.noauth: self.app_noauth,\n            PvfAppTarget.session: self.app_session,\n            PvfAppTarget.share: self.app_share,\n            PvfAppTarget.external: self.app_external,\n        }[PvfAppTarget(target)]\n\n\n# The runner records the active invocation here so pvf internals (share gate, webhook\n# delivery, ...) can reach registered hooks at request time without importing the app.\ninvocation_context: PvfInvocation = None\n\ndef set_current_invocation(invocation: PvfInvocation | None) -> None:\n    global invocation_context\n    invocation_context = invocation\n\n\ndef get_current_invocation() -> PvfInvocation | None:\n    return invocation_context\n\n\ndef get_hooks() -> PvfHookRegistry:\n    invocation = invocation_context\n    return invocation.hooks if invocation is not None else PvfHookRegistry()\n\n\ndef runtime_settings() -> BaseModel:\n    \"\"\"Active settings for pvf request paths: the app instance when bound, else pvf_settings.\"\"\"\n    invocation = invocation_context\n    if invocation is not None and invocation.app_settings is not None:\n        return invocation.app_settings\n    return pvf_settings\n","lineStart":1,"lineEnd":148,"totalLines":148,"truncated":false}},"title":"src/pvf/bindings/pvf_invocation.py","time":{"start":1787455186668,"end":1787455187087}},"metadata":{"openai":{"itemId":"fc_01a02ca1a38d743082104a2792ff9c20"}},"id":"prt_02ca1a3e6001EHq62v3X5L8d6W","sessionID":"ses_fd35ea0e5ffe34F6Skn5PFxymm","messageID":"msg_02ca19b66001dCjXDZNitUJH0H"}}

{ mdAndUp } = useDisplay()\nconst { initials } = useFormat()\nconst { isDark, toggle: toggleTheme } = useThemeMode()\nconst drawer = ref(true)\nconst aboutOpen = ref(false)\nconst versionCheck = },\n]\n\nconst pageTitle = computed(() => {\n  const projectRest = route.path.match(/^\\/projects\\/[^/]+(?:\\/(.*))?$/)\n  if (projectRest) {\n    const rest = projectRest[1] || ''\n    if (!rest) Format: code_1:d-abc,code_n:d-xyz\"\"\"\n29:     if raw_value is None or not str(raw_value).strip():\n30:         return {}\n31:     template_ids: dict[str, str] = {}\n32:     for pair in raw_value.split(\",\"):\n33:         pair = pair.strip()\n34:         if not pair or \":\" not in pair:\n35:             continue\n36:         code, sg_id = pair.split(\":\", 1)\n37:         code = code.strip()\n38:         sg_id = sg_id.strip()\n39:         if code:\n40:             template_ids[code] = sg_id\n41:     return template_ids\n42: \n43: def parse_csv_name_list(raw_value: str | list | None) -> list[str]:\n44:     \"\"\"Parse a comma-separated name list. Empty string or None becomes []. Already-a-list is stripped.\"\"\"\n45:     if raw_value is None:\n46:         return []\n47:     if isinstance(raw_value, list):\n48:         return [str(token).strip() for token in raw_value if str(token).strip()]\n49:     text = str(raw_value)\n50:     if not text.strip():\n51:         return []\n52:     return [token.strip() for token in text.split(\",\") if token.strip()]\n53: \n54: def get_trial_account_codes_dict(env_var_name, default_value):\n55:     \"\"\" retrieve trial_code_list from default or environment value by env_var_name or its upper case, converted via typ_var, or default value\n56:         format is: code_1:days,code_n:days\n57:     \"\"\"\n58:     if env_var_name not in environ:\n59:         env_var_name = env_var_name.upper()\n60:     if env_var_name in environ:\n61:         get_value = environ.get(env_var_name)\n62:     else:\n63:         get_value = default_value\n64: \n65:     trial_code_list = [x.strip() for x in get_value.split(',') if x.strip() != '']\n66:     trial_code_dict = {}\n67:     for trial_code in trial_code_list:\n68:         trial_code_parts = [x.strip().lower() for x in trial_code.split(':', 2)]\n69:         code = trial_code_parts[0]\n70:         days = int(trial_code_parts[1])\n71:         trial_code_dict[code] = days\n72:     # print('Loaded trial account codes: ', trial_code_dict)\n73:     return trial_code_dict\n74: \n75: class PvfGlobalSettings(BaseSettings):\n76:     PVF_VERSION: str = \"1.2.0\"\n77:     APPLICATION_NAME: str = environ.get(\"APPLICATION_NAME\", \"PV Framework App Name\")\n78: \n79:     model_config = SettingsConfigDict(env_file=path.join(pvf_env_dir, \".env\"))\n80:     server_env: str = \"local\"\n81:     computer_name: str = gethostname()\n82:     environment_name: str = environ.get(\"ENVIRONMENT_NAME\", \"local-dev\")\n83:     process_name: str = \"_not_specified_\"\n84:     process_id: int = getpid()\n85: \n86:     PYTEST_ACTIVE: bool = bool(int(environ.get(\"PYTEST_ACTIVE\", 0)))\n87:     UNIQUE_CONFIGURATION_CHECK: bool = bool(int(environ.get(\"UNIQUE_CONFIGURATION_CHECK\", 0)))  # only set to 1 in local .env, always 0 in example.env, so a check that env is custom\n88: \n89:     APPLICATION_BASE_URL: str = environ.get(\"APPLICATION_BASE_URL\", \"http://localhost:9999\")\n90:     APPLICATION_SERVER_PORT: int = int(environ.get(\"APPLICATION_SERVER_PORT\", 9999))\n91:     APP_LOGIN_PATH: str = environ.get(\"APP_LOGIN_PATH\", \"login\")\n92:     APP_RESET_PASSWORD_URL: str = environ.get(\"APP_RESET_PASSWORD_URL\", \"forgot-password\")\n93: \n94:     # --- Startup-owned values (pvf_app_startup.yaml) -------------------------\n95:     # Deployment-fixed feature switches and share vocabularies. \"\n128:                 \"only the pvf startup logic may set it\"\n129:             )\n130:         super().__setattr__(name, value)\n131: \n132:     def _inject_startup_values(self, values: dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n133:         \"\"\"Framework startup use only: apply values loaded from pvf_app_startup.yaml.\n134: \n135:         Bypasses the read-only guard deliberately.
