get_env(type_var, env_var_name, default_value):\n    \"\"\" retrieve environment value by env_var_name or its upper case, converted via typ_var, or default value\n    \"\"\"\n    if env_var_name not in environ:\n        env_var_name = env_var_name.upper()\n    if env_var_name in environ:\n        get_value = environ.get(env_var_name)\n        if type_var is not None:\n            get_value = type_var(get_value)\n    else:\n        get_value = default_value\n    return get_value\n\ndef parse_sendgrid_template_ids(raw_value: str | None) -> dict[str, str]:\n    \"\"\"Parse template_code:sendgrid_id pairs. Format: code_1:d-abc,code_n:d-xyz\"\"\"\n    if raw_value is None or not str(raw_value).strip():\n        return {}\n    template_ids: dict[str, str] = {}\n    for pair in raw_value.split(\",\"):\n        pair = pair.strip()\n        if not pair or \":\" not in pair:\n            continue\n        code, sg_id = pair.split(\":\", 1)\n        code = code.strip()\n        sg_id = sg_id.strip()\n        if code:\n            template_ids[code] = sg_id\n    return template_ids\n\ndef parse_csv_name_list(raw_value: str | list | None) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"Parse a comma-separated name list. Empty string or None becomes []. Already-a-list is stripped.\"\"\"\n    if raw_value is None:\n        return []\n    if isinstance(raw_value, list):\n        return [str(token).strip() for token in raw_value if str(token).strip()]\n    text = str(raw_value)\n    if not text.strip():\n        return []\n    return [token.strip() for token in text.split(\",\") if token.strip()]\n\ndef get_trial_account_codes_dict(env_var_name, default_value):\n    \"\"\" retrieve trial_code_list from default or environment value by env_var_name or its upper case, converted via typ_var, or default value\n        format is: code_1:days,code_n:days\n    \"\"\"\n    if env_var_name not in environ:\n        env_var_name = env_var_name.upper()\n    if env_var_name in environ:\n        get_value = environ.get(env_var_name)\n    else:\n        get_value = default_value\n\n    trial_code_list = [x.strip() for x in get_value.split(',') if x.strip() != '']\n    trial_code_dict = {}\n    for trial_code in trial_code_list:\n        trial_code_parts = [x.strip().lower() for x in trial_code.split(':', 2)]\n        code = trial_code_parts[0]\n        days = int(trial_code_parts[1])\n        trial_code_dict[code] = days\n    # print('Loaded trial account codes: ', trial_code_dict)\n    return trial_code_dict\n\nclass PvfGlobalSettings(BaseSettings):\n    PVF_VERSION: str = \"1.2.0\"\n    APPLICATION_NAME: str = environ.get(\"APPLICATION_NAME\", \"PV Framework App Name\")\n\n    model_config = SettingsConfigDict(env_file=path.join(pvf_env_dir, \".env\"))\n    server_env: str = \"local\"\n    computer_name: str = gethostname()\n    environment_name: str = environ.get(\"ENVIRONMENT_NAME\", \"local-dev\")\n    process_name: str = \"_not_specified_\"\n    process_id: int = getpid()\n\n    PYTEST_ACTIVE: bool = bool(int(environ.get(\"PYTEST_ACTIVE\", 0)))\n    UNIQUE_CONFIGURATION_CHECK: bool = bool(int(environ.get(\"UNIQUE_CONFIGURATION_CHECK\", 0)))  # only set to 1 in local .env, always 0 in example.env, so a check that env is custom\n\n    APPLICATION_BASE_URL: str = environ.get(\"APPLICATION_BASE_URL\", \"http://localhost:9999\")\n    APPLICATION_SERVER_PORT: int = int(environ.get(\"APPLICATION_SERVER_PORT\", 9999))\n    APP_LOGIN_PATH: str = environ.get(\"APP_LOGIN_PATH\", \"login\")\n    APP_RESET_PASSWORD_URL: str = environ.get(\"APP_RESET_PASSWORD_URL\", \"forgot-password\")\n\n    # --- Startup-owned values (pvf_app_startup.yaml) -------------------------\n    # Deployment-fixed feature switches and share vocabularies. These are set\n    # ONLY by the pvf startup logic from pvf_app_startup.yaml (see\n    # pvf.config.pvf_startup_config.apply_startup_config). They are declared\n    # here as informational dummies; being ClassVars, pydantic-settings will\n    # never populate them from .env or process environment, and __setattr__\n    # below rejects any ordinary assignment.\n    ACTIVATE_STRIPE_INTEGRATION: ClassVar[bool] = False\n    ACTIVATE_BRANDING_IMAGE_STORE: ClassVar[bool] = False\n    ACTIVATE_SHARE_LINKS: ClassVar[bool] = True\n    ACTIVATE_EXTERNAL_API: ClassVar[bool] = True\n    ACTIVATE_CUSTOMER_COMMUNICATION: ClassVar[bool] = True\n    ACTIVATE_AI_AGENTS: ClassVar[bool] = False\n    # Share link vocabularies (normalized to plain string lists at injection).\n    # Defaults match the historical powerchoice values so import-time consumers\n    # behave unchanged before startup injection runs.\n    SHARE_OBJECT_ACTIONS: ClassVar[list[str]] = [\"not_set\", \"vote\", \"vote_view\", \"report\"]\n    SHARE_ACCESS_OPERATIONS: ClassVar[list[str]] = [\"not_set\", \"vote\", \"view\", \"report\"]\n\n    _STARTUP_VALUE_NAMES: ClassVar[frozenset[str]] = frozenset({\n        \"ACTIVATE_STRIPE_INTEGRATION\",\n        \"ACTIVATE_BRANDING_IMAGE_STORE\",\n        \"ACTIVATE_SHARE_LINKS\",\n        \"ACTIVATE_EXTERNAL_API\",\n        \"ACTIVATE_CUSTOMER_COMMUNICATION\",\n        \"ACTIVATE_AI_AGENTS\",\n        \"SHARE_OBJECT_ACTIONS\",\n        \"SHARE_ACCESS_OPERATIONS\",\n    })\n\n    def __setattr__(self, name: str, value: Any) -> None:\n        if name in self._STARTUP_VALUE_NAMES:\n            raise RuntimeError(\n                f\"{name} is owned by pvf_app_startup.yaml and is read-only; \"\n                \"only the pvf startup logic may set it\"\n            )\n        super().__setattr__(name, value)\n\n    def _inject_startup_values(self, values: dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Framework startup use only: apply values loaded from pvf_app_startup.yaml.\n\n        Bypasses the read-only guard deliberately. Not part of the application-facing API.\n        \"\"\"\n        for name, value in values.items():\n            if name not in self._STARTUP_VALUE_NAMES:\n                raise KeyError(f\"{name} is not a startup-owned setting\")\n            object.__setattr__(self, name, value)\n\n    BOOTSTRAP_SAMPLE_DATA: int = int(environ.get(\"BOOTSTRAP_SAMPLE_DATA\", False))\n    BOOTSTRAP_CUSTOMER_EMAIL: str = environ.get(\"BOOTSTRAP_CUSTOMER_EMAIL\", None)\n    BOOTSTRAP_ADMIN_USER_EMAIL: str = environ.get(\"BOOTSTRAP_ADMIN_USER_EMAIL\", None)\n    BOOTSTRAP_ADMIN_PASSWORD: str = environ.get(\"BOOTSTRAP_ADMIN_PASSWORD\", None)\n    BOOTSTRAP_NON_ADMIN_USER_EMAIL: str = environ.get(\"BOOTSTRAP_NON_ADMIN_USER_EMAIL\", None)\n    BOOTSTRAP_NON_ADMIN_PASSWORD: str = environ.get(\"BOOTSTRAP_NON_ADMIN_PASSWORD\", None)\n\n    APPLICATION_ACCOUNT_GRACE_PERIOD_DAYS: int = int(environ.get(\"APPLICATION_ACCOUNT_GRACE_PERIOD_DAYS\", 3))   # provides this many days after expiration to keep service active\n    APPLICATION_ACCOUNT_TRIAL_GRACE_PERIOD_DAYS: int = int(environ.get(\"APPLICATION_ACCOUNT_TRIAL_GRACE_PERIOD_DAYS\", 3))   # provides this many days after expiration to keep service active\n\n    PLATFORM_LINUX: int = int(environ.get(\"PLATFORM_LINUX\", 1))\n    DB_PATH_OR_CONNECTION_STRING: str = environ.get(\"DB_PATH_OR_CONNECTION_STRING\", \"sqlite:///local.db\")    # Default to creating a local database\n    DB_ECHO_SQL_TO_CONSOLE: bool = bool(int(environ.get(\"DB_ECHO_SQL_TO_CONSOLE\", 0)))\n\n    # Token secrets and settings\n    JWT_SECRET_KEY:str = environ.get(\"JWT_SECRET_KEY\", \"enk1xmalq8pdnkb902x3hzo0uo9ayyoaubt2da57ucvh8pebelvfauyej2bzdak6jieso81ri\")\n    JWT_REFRESH_SECRET_KEY:str = environ.get(\"JWT_REFRESH\", \"12veui23ohkwanuignobggxbws9x5zxs4g8c29fxgb4d3gb7tl7vsz31p9uc3m0al82fitxbgedv\")\n    ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES:int = int(environ.get(\"ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES\", 10080))\n    JWT_ALGORITHM: str = environ.get(\"JWT_ALGORITHM\", \"HS256\")\n    CLEARED_TOKEN_VALUE: str = environ.get(\"CLEARED_TOKEN_VALUE\", \"I'm no longer here, Where did I go?\")\n    PW_RESET_FLOODING_LIMIT_SECONDS: int = int(environ.get(\"PW_RESET_FLOODING_LIMIT_SECONDS\", 90))\n    PW_RESET_TOKEN_VALID_SECONDS: int = int(environ.get(\"PW_RESET_TOKEN_VALID_SECONDS\", 7200))\n    ACCOUNT_ACTIVATION_TOKEN_KEY: str = environ.get(\"ACCOUNT_ACTIVATION_TOKEN_KEY\", \"2b04wgwt4x4xvu2m6u3rfd\")\n\n    # Items with weird values (like TRIAL_ACCOUNT_CODES) need custom field validators to override the default\n    # parser.  Otherwise Pydanting will try to parse this itself with a JSON validator and fail.\n    # The default must be set to None to get the validator to run.\n\n    # TRIAL_ACCOUNT_CODES: dict[str, Tuple[int, int]] = get_trial_account_codes_dict(\"TRIAL_ACCOUNT_CODES\", \"trial_account_30_days:30:100\")  # sample, 30 day trial with 100 tokens granted with same expiration; set with =code:days:tokens,code:days:tokens,... becomes key = (days, tokens)\n    CUSTOMER_ACTIVATION_URL: str = \"activate-customer\"\n    TRIAL_ACCOUNT_CODES: dict[str, int] | None = None  # sample, days of trial for a given trial code. ; example trial_code_1:10,...,trial_code_N:30\n    @field_validator(\"TRIAL_ACCOUNT_CODES\", mode=\"before\")\n    def parse_trial_account_codes(cls, value):\n        \"\"\"Custom parser for TRIAL_ACCOUNT_CODES.\"\"\"\n        if isinstance(value, str):\n            return get_trial_account_codes_dict(\"TRIAL_ACCOUNT_CODES\", value)\n        return get_trial_account_codes_dict(\"TRIAL_ACCOUNT_CODES\", \"trial_account_30_days:30:100\")\n\n    # Comma-separated field names stripped from user_record and customer_record on context/session\n    # responses (multiple values allowed, e.g. stripe_settings,client_settings). Empty string or None\n    # disables masking. Unset uses the default stripe_settings. Converted to list[str] once at load.\n    CONTEXT_MASKED_FIELDS: list[str] | None = None\n    @field_validator(\"CONTEXT_MASKED_FIELDS\", mode=\"before\")\n    def parse_context_masked_fields(cls, value):\n        \"\"\"Custom parser for CONTEXT_MASKED_FIELDS.\"\"\"\n        if value is None:\n            return [\"stripe_settings\"]\n        return parse_csv_name_list(value)\n\n    ACCESS_MAGIC_KEY_EXPIRATION_MINUTES: int = int(environ.get(\"ACCESS_MAGIC_KEY_EXPIRATION_MINUTES\", 70))\n    ACCESS_MAGIC_KEY_EXPIRATION_MESSAGE: str = environ.get(\"ACCESS_MAGIC_KEY_EXPIRATION_MESSAGE\", \"one hour\")\n\n    LOGIN_2FA_MODE: str = environ.get(\"LOGIN_2FA_MODE\", \"disabled\") # disabled / sysadmins / admins / admins+optin / all\n    LOGIN_2FA_VALIDITY_MINUTES: int = int(environ.get(\"LOGIN_2FA_VALIDITY_MINUTES\", 15))\n    LOGIN_2FA_VALIDITY_MESSAGE: str = environ.get(\"LOGIN_2FA_VALIDITY_MESSAGE\", \"15 minutes\")\n    LOGIN_2FA_RESEND_LIMIT_SECONDS: int = int(environ.get(\"LOGIN_2FA_RESEND_LIMIT_SECONDS\", 90))\n    token_security_digits_only: bool = bool(int(environ.get(\"TOKEN_SECURITY_DIGITS_ONLY\", 0)))\n\n    # Share link support (framework-generic; share type/action vocabularies are startup-owned above)\n    VIEW_TOKEN_KEY: str = environ.get(\"VIEW_TOKEN_KEY\", \"view-token-key-change-in-env\")\n    ACCESS_VIEW_COOKIE: str = environ.get(\"ACCESS_VIEW_COOKIE\", \"pc_share_view\")\n    SHARE_LINK_REST_PW_HASH_KEY: str = environ.get(\"SHARE_LINK_REST_PW_HASH_KEY\", \"x6q9uuwmagcy717wioctg6\")\n    SHARE_LINK_EXPIRATION_DAYS: int = int(environ.get(\"SHARE_LINK_EXPIRATION_DAYS\", 30))\n    SHARE_LINK_COOKIE_EXPIRATION_DAYS: int = int(environ.get(\"SHARE_LINK_COOKIE_EXPIRATION_DAYS\", 14))\n    SHARE_LINK_EXPIRATION_MESSAGE: str = environ.get(\"SHARE_LINK_EXPIRATION_MESSAGE\", \"30 days\")\n    SHARE_GATE_THROTTLE_WINDOW_SECONDS: int = int(environ.get(\"SHARE_GATE_THROTTLE_WINDOW_SECONDS\", 3600))\n    SHARE_GATE_MAX_UNAUTH_PER_IP_PER_HOUR: int = int(environ.get(\"SHARE_GATE_MAX_UNAUTH_PER_IP_PER_HOUR\", 60))\n    SHARE_GATE_MAX_FAILURES_PER_IP_PER_HOUR: int = int(environ.get(\"SHARE_GATE_MAX_FAILURES_PER_IP_PER_HOUR\", 20))\n    SHARE_GATE_MAX_FAILURES_PER_TOKEN_PER_HOUR: int = int(environ.get(\"SHARE_GATE_MAX_FAILURES_PER_TOKEN_PER_HOUR\", 10))\n\n    SHARE_PROJECT_APP_VIEW_BASE_URL: str = \"/share/\"   # must match client share landing routes\n    SHARE_PROJECT_APP_VIEW_SUFFIX_URL: str = \"\"   # optional suffix after magic token\n    SHARE_WS_BASE_URL: str = \"/ext-ws/share/\"  # API path prefix used for share cookies\n    # Security notes included in share invitation emails (plain text guidance for recipients)\n    SHARE_SECURITY_PASSWORD_INCLUDED_NOTE: str = \"A password is required to open this link. Password: {share_password}\"\n    SHARE_SECURITY_PASSWORD_SEPARATE_NOTE: str = \"A password is required to open this link. The password was provided separately.\"\n    SHARE_SECURITY_NOTE_OPEN_ACCESS: str = \"This link is open access; no additional verification is required.\"\n    SHARE_SECURITY_NOTE_EMAIL_ANY_UNVERIFIED: str = \"You will be asked to enter an email address to continue.\"\n    SHARE_SECURITY_NOTE_EMAIL_ANY_VERIFIED: str = \"You will verify any email address via a one-time access key.\"\n    SHARE_SECURITY_NOTE_EMAIL_MATCHING: str = \"You must enter the email address this link was sent to.\"\n    SHARE_SECURITY_NOTE_EMAIL_MATCHING_VERIFIED: str = \"You must verify the email address this link was sent to via a one-time access key.\"\n    SHARE_SECURITY_NOTE_RECIPIENT_EMAIL_VERIFIED: str = \"A one-time access key will be sent to the email on file for this link.\"\n    SHARE_SECURITY_NOTE_PASSWORD_ONLY: str = \"A password is required to open this link.\"\n    SHARE_SECURITY_NOTE_PASSWORD_WITH_EMAIL_ANY_UNVERIFIED: str = \"A password and an email address are required to open this link.\"\n    SHARE_SECURITY_NOTE_PASSWORD_WITH_EMAIL_ANY_VERIFIED: str = \"A password is required, then any email is verified via a one-time access key.\"\n    SHARE_SECURITY_NOTE_PASSWORD_WITH_EMAIL_MATCHING: str = \"A password and the original recipient email are required.\"\n    SHARE_SECURITY_NOTE_PASSWORD_WITH_EMAIL_MATCHING_VERIFIED: str = \"A password is required, then the original recipient email is verified via a one-time access key.\"\n    SHARE_SECURITY_NOTE_PASSWORD_WITH_RECIPIENT_EMAIL_VERIFIED: str = \"A password is required, then a one-time access key is sent to the email on file.\"\n\n    # UI option gating for share access modes. These flags limit the choices offered in the\n    # client only; the backend gate supports every access mode regardless, so an application\n    # can tailor options to its audience without disabling functionality.\n    ENABLE_SHARE_OPEN_ACCESS : bool = bool(int(environ.get(\"ENABLE_SHARE_OPEN_ACCESS\", 0)))                      # Access Url; Enter Name; Access granted.\n    ENABLE_SHARE_EMAIL_ANY_UNVERIFIED : bool = bool(int(environ.get(\"ENABLE_SHARE_EMAIL_ANY_UNVERIFIED\", 1)))   # Access Url; Enter Name & Any Email (not verified); Access Granted.\n    ENABLE_SHARE_EMAIL_ANY_VERIFIED : bool = bool(int(environ.get(\"ENABLE_SHARE_EMAIL_ANY_VERIFIED\", 1)))       # Access Url; Enter Name & Any Email; Token email sent; Follow link or enter key from token email; Access Granted.\n    ENABLE_SHARE_EMAIL_MATCHING : bool = bool(int(environ.get(\"ENABLE_SHARE_EMAIL_MATCHING\", 1)))               # Access Url; Enter Name & Email; Email must match share; Access Granted.\n    ENABLE_SHARE_EMAIL_MATCHING_VERIFIED : bool = bool(int(environ.get(\"ENABLE_SHARE_EMAIL_MATCHING_VERIFIED\", 0)))   # Access Url; Enter Name & Email; Email must match share; Token email sent; Use link or key to verify; Access Granted.\n    ENABLE_SHARE_RECIPIENT_EMAIL_VERIFIED : bool = bool(int(environ.get(\"ENABLE_SHARE_RECIPIENT_EMAIL_VERIFIED\", 1))) # Access Url; Enter Name Only; Token email sent (based on share value); Use link or key to verify; Access Granted.\n    ENABLE_SHARE_PASSWORD_ONLY : bool = bool(int(environ.get(\"ENABLE_SHARE_PASSWORD_ONLY\", 0)))                        # Access Url; Enter Name Only; Password required from share; Access Granted.\n    ENABLE_SHARE_PASSWORD_WITH_EMAIL_ANY_UNVERIFIED : bool = bool(int(environ.get(\"ENABLE_SHARE_PASSWORD_WITH_EMAIL_ANY_UNVERIFIED\", 1)))   # Access Url; Enter Name & Any Email (not verified); Password required from share; Access Granted.\n    ENABLE_SHARE_PASSWORD_WITH_EMAIL_ANY_VERIFIED : bool = bool(int(environ.get(\"ENABLE_SHARE_PASSWORD_WITH_EMAIL_ANY_VERIFIED\", 0)))       # Access Url; Enter Name & Any Email (verified); Password required from share; Token email sent; Use link or key to verify; Access Granted.\n    ENABLE_SHARE_PASSWORD_WITH_EMAIL_MATCHING: bool = bool(int(environ.get(\"ENABLE_SHARE_PASSWORD_WITH_EMAIL_MATCHING\", 0)))          # Access Url; Enter Name & Email; Email must match share; Password required from share; Access Granted.\n    ENABLE_SHARE_PASSWORD_WITH_EMAIL_MATCHING_VERIFIED : bool = bool(int(environ.get(\"ENABLE_SHARE_PASSWORD_WITH_EMAIL_MATCHING_VERIFIED\", 0)))   # Access Url; Enter Name & Email; Email must match share; Password required from share; Token email sent; Use link or key to verify; Access Granted.\n    ENABLE_SHARE_PASSWORD_WITH_RECIPIENT_EMAIL_VERIFIED : bool = bool(int(environ.get(\"ENABLE_SHARE_PASSWORD_WITH_RECIPIENT_EMAIL_VERIFIED\", 0))) # Access Url; Enter Name Only; Password required from share; Token email sent (based on share value); Use link or key to verify; Access Granted.\n\n    # External (customer toolkit) API signing\n    CUSTOMER_API_INBOUND_HASH_KEY: str = environ.get(\"CUSTOMER_API_INBOUND_HASH_KEY\", \"265c0a4a9832bcd2c7ffd8eb89dc11ca\")\n    CUSTOMER_API_OUTBOUND_HASH_KEY: str = environ.get(\"CUSTOMER_API_OUTBOUND_HASH_KEY\", \"7aa7b7a445b96cdf2f5c701854b8af9c\")\n    CUSTOMER_API_TIME_TOLERANCE: int = int(environ.get(\"CUSTOMER_API_TIME_TOLERANCE\", 4800))   # 80 minutes\n\n    # Watcher process (callback delivery and other background modules)\n    WATCHER_PROCESS_MODULE_LIST: list[str] = [x.strip() for x in environ.get(\"WATCHER_PROCESS_MODULE_LIST\", \"\").split(',')]\n    WATCHER_PROCESS_SHOW_API_CALLBACKS: bool = bool(int(environ.get(\"WATCHER_PROCESS_SHOW_API_CALLBACKS\", 0)))  # if true, the watcher will display API callback summary as processed\n    WATCHER_PROCESS_API_CALLBACKS: bool = bool(int(environ.get(\"WATCHER_PROCESS_API_CALLBACKS\", 1)))  # if true, the watcher will process and deliver eligible API callbacks\n    WATCHER_PROCESS_API_CALLBACKS_TAG: str = environ.get(\"WATCHER_PROCESS_API_CALLBACKS_TAG\", \"process_api_callbacks\")  # tag for surrogate module types used in the watcher\n    WATCHER_PROCESS_API_CALLBACKS_MAXIMUM_ATTEMPTS: int = int(environ.get(\"WATCHER_PROCESS_API_CALLBACKS_MAXIMUM_ATTEMPTS\", 5))  # maximum number of attempts to deliver an API callback before it is considered failed\n    WATCHER_PROCESS_API_CALLBACKS_RETRY_DELAYS: list[int] = [int(x.strip()) for x in environ.get(\"WATCHER_PROCESS_API_CALLBACKS_RETRY_DELAYS\", \"1,4,10,60,60,120,120,120,120,120\").split(',')]\n    WATCHER_SHORTER_BUSY_POLL_SLEEP: float = float(environ.get(\"WATCHER_SHORTER_BUSY_POLL_SLEEP\", 1.0))  # seconds between polls while work is being found\n    WATCHER_LONGER_IDLE_INCREASING_POLL_SLEEP: float = float(environ.get(\"WATCHER_LONGER_IDLE_INCREASING_POLL_SLEEP\", 5.0))  # base seconds between polls once idle; grows by the exponential factor\n    WATCHER_POLL_EXPONENTIAL_FACTOR: float = float(environ.get(\"WATCHER_POLL_EXPONENTIAL_FACTOR\", 1.2))  # idle poll backoff multiplier\n    WATCHER_POLL_MAXIMUM_SLEEP_TIME: float = float(environ.get(\"WATCHER_POLL_MAXIMUM_SLEEP_TIME\", 30.0))  # cap on seconds between polls\n    WATCHER_PROCESS_EMAIL_LISTENER_ENABLE: bool = bool(int(environ.get(\"WATCHER_PROCESS_EMAIL_LISTENER_ENABLE\", 0)))  # reserved; no email listener processing is implemented\n    WATCHER_PROCESS_EMAIL_LISTENER_TAG: str = environ.get(\"WATCHER_PROCESS_EMAIL_LISTENER_TAG\", \"process_email_listener\")  # tag for surrogate module types used for email listener\n    WATCHER_PROCESS_AI_AGENTS_TAG: str = environ.get(\"WATCHER_PROCESS_AI_AGENTS_TAG\", \"process_ai_agents\")\n    WATCHER_EMAIL_LISTENER_SUBMISSION_ADDRESS: str = environ.get(\"WATCHER_EMAIL_LISTENER_SUBMISSION_ADDRESS\", \"\")\n\n    MAX_REPORTS_PER_DAY: int = int(environ.get(\"MAX_REPORTS_PER_DAY\", 8))\n\n    # \n    USE_EMAIL_SERVICE: str | None = environ.get(\"USE_EMAIL_SERVICE\", None) # None disables outbound email; SMTP-JINJA uses local templates and defined SMTP; SENDGRID uses sendgrid settings, account, and templates\n    EMAIL_LOCAL_ALLOW_DOMAINS: str = environ.get(\"EMAIL_LOCAL_ALLOW_DOMAINS\", \"mailinator.com\")\n    EMAIL_BRAND_NAME: str = environ.get(\"EMAIL_BRAND_NAME\", \"Power Choice Pro\")\n\n    EMAIL_SUPPORT_EMAIL: str = environ.get(\"EMAIL_SUPPORT_EMAIL\", \"\")\n    MAX_EMAILS_PER_DEST_PER_HOUR: int = int(environ.get(\"MAX_EMAILS_PER_DEST_PER_HOUR\", 4))\n    MAX_EMAILS_PER_DEST_PER_24_HOURS: int = int(environ.get(\"MAX_EMAILS_PER_DEST_PER_24_HOURS\", 8))\n    MAX_EMAILS_PER_REMOTE_IP_PER_HOUR_VERIFIED: int = int(environ.get(\"MAX_EMAILS_PER_REMOTE_IP_PER_HOUR_VERIFIED\", 20))\n    MAX_EMAILS_PER_REMOTE_IP_PER_HOUR: int = int(environ.get(\"MAX_EMAILS_PER_REMOTE_IP_PER_HOUR\", 4))\n\n    # Settings to use a direct SMTP sender.\n    DIRECT_EMAIL_FROM_NAME: str = environ.get(\"DIRECT_EMAIL_FROM_NAME\", 'Service')\n    DIRECT_EMAIL_FROM_ADDRESS: str = environ.get(\"DIRECT_EMAIL_FROM_ADDRESS\", 'no_reply@domain.com')\n    DIRECT_EMAIL_SMTP_HOST: str = environ.get(\"DIRECT_EMAIL_SMTP_HOST\", \"smtp.example.com\")\n    DIRECT_EMAIL_SMTP_PORT: int = int(environ.get(\"DIRECT_EMAIL_SMTP_PORT\", 587)) # often 465 for SSL\n    DIRECT_EMAIL_SMTP_TLS: bool = bool(int(environ.get(\"DIRECT_EMAIL_SMTP_TLS\", 1)))\n    DIRECT_EMAIL_SMTP_SSL: bool = bool(int(environ.get(\"DIRECT_EMAIL_SMTP_SSL\", 1)))\n    DIRECT_EMAIL_SMTP_USER: str = environ.get(\"DIRECT_EMAIL_SMTP_USER\", \"\")\n    DIRECT_EMAIL_SMTP_PASSWORD: str = environ.get(\"DIRECT_EMAIL_SMTP_PASSWORD\", \"\")\n    DIRECT_EMAIL_SMTP_TIMEOUT: int = int(environ.get(\"DIRECT_EMAIL_SMTP_TIMEOUT\", 20))\n    DIRECT_TEMPLATE_DIRECTORY: str = environ.get(\"DIRECT_TEMPLATE_DIRECTORY\", \"../email_templates\")\n\n    #   return raw\n    if isinstance(raw, str):\n        try:\n            data = json.loads(raw)\n            return data if isinstance(data, dict) else {}\n        except Exception:\n            return {}\n    return {}\n\n\ndef _client_settings_defaults():\n    inv = invocation_context\n    if inv is not None and inv.app_client_settings is not None:\n        return copy.deepcopy(inv.app_client_settings)\n    settings_type = inv.client_session_context_settings_type if inv is not None else None\n    if settings_type is not None:\n        return settings_type()\n    return PvfClientAuthSettings()\n\n\ndef _user_communication_available() -> bool:\n    return bool(getattr(runtime_settings(), \"ACTIVATE_CUSTOMER_COMMUNICATION\", False))\n\n\ndef _ai_yaml_activated() -> bool:\n    return bool(getattr(runtime_settings(), \"ACTIVATE_AI_AGENTS\", False))\n\n\ndef _ai_features_available() -> bool:\n    return _ai_yaml_activated()\n\n\ndef _customer_llm_configured(customer) -> bool:\n    if customer is None:\n        return False\n    try:\n        return bool(customer.customer_llm_configured())\n    except Exception:\n        provider = str(getattr(customer, \"ai_provider\", \"\") or \"\").strip()\n        key = decrypt_secret(getattr(customer, \"ai_api_key\", None) or \"\").strip()\n        return bool(provider) and provider_is_supported(provider) and bool(key)\n\n\ndef _ai_providers_configured(customer=None) -> bool:\n    if not _ai_yaml_activated():\n        return False\n    return system_llm_configured() or _customer_llm_configured(customer)\n\n\ndef _active_version() -> str | None:\n    try:\n        inv = invocation_context\n        if inv is not None and getattr(inv, \"application_version\", None):\n            return str(inv.application_version)\n    except Exception:\n        pass\n    try:\n        rs = runtime_settings()\n        v = getattr(rs, \"VERSION\", None)\n        if v:\n            return str(v)\n    except Exception:\n        pass\n    try:\n        from ..config.pvf_config_settings import pvf_settings as _pvs\n        v2 = getattr(_pvs, \"PVF_VERSION\", None)\n        if v2:\n            return str(v2)\n    except Exception:\n        pass\n    return None\n\n\ndef _merged_client_settings(*blobs):\n    merged = _client_settings_defaults()\n    for raw in blobs:\n        data = _parse_settings_blob(raw)\n        for k, v in data.items():\n            if hasattr(merged, k):\n                setattr(merged, k, v)\n    if hasattr(merged, \"enable_user_communication\"):\n        merged.enable_user_communication = _user_communication_available()\n    if hasattr(merged, \"ai_features_enabled\"):\n        merged.ai_features_enabled = _ai_features_available()\n    if hasattr(merged, \"ai_providers_configured\"):\n        merged.ai_providers_configured = False\n    return merged\n\n\nclass RequestUserCustomerContext(BaseModel):\n    include_account_status: bool = customer_id: int = Field(default=-1, description=\"system assigned id when customer was created\")\n    customer_name: str | None = Field(default=None, description=\"customer's business name\")\n    customer_email: str | None = Field(default=None, description=\"provided email for the customer level\")\n    stripe_customer_id: str | None = Field(default=None, description=\"Indicates customer          if extra not in masked_fields:\n                masked_fields.append(extra)\n        if not masked_fields:\n            return data\n        for record_key in (\"user_record\", record.pop(field_name, None)\n        return data\n\n@noauth_router.post(\"/login-and-get-context\",\n             summary=\"Create access and refresh tokens for user session and return session Login2FAChallenge:\n    user_result = await auth_request(response=response, request=request, session=session, login=login, return_user_info=True)\n    if isinstance(user_result, Login2FAChallenge):\n        return user_result\n    return get_user_customer_context(session=session, usr_context=UserContext(authenticated_login=True, sess_user=user_result, remote_ip=request.client.host, cur_customer\n    else:\n        cur_customer = usr_context.sess_customer\n\n    trial_expiration_days = None\n    if cur_customer.customer_account in (None, \"\") and cur_customer.trial_expiration_date is not None:\n        check_expiration_date = cur_customer.trial_expiration_date\n        check_expiration_date = check_expiration_date.astimezone(timezone.utc)\n\n        check_trial_expiration_days = (check_expiration_date - datetime.now(timezone.utc)).days\n        if check_trial_expiration_days >= 0:\n            trial_expiration_days = check_trial_expiration_days\n\n    _active = _active_version()\n    context_view = UserCustomerContextView(context_timestamp=datetime.now(timezone.utc),\n {trial_expiration_days} days',\n                                             stripe_customer_id=usr_context.sess_customer.customer_account,\n                                             stripe_callback_prefix=runtime_settings().STRIPE_CALLBACK_PREFIX,\n                                             stripe_callback_prefix_sandbox=runtime_settings().STRIPE_CALLBACK_PREFIX_SANDBOX,\n                                             trial_expiration_days=trial_expiration_days,\n                                             is_customer_admin=usr_context.sess_user.customer_admin,\n                                             user_record=usr_context.sess_user,\n                                             customer_record=cur_customer,\n                                             server_version=_active,\n                                             client_version=_active,\n                                             )\n    context_view.customer_name = cur_customer.customer_name\n    context_view.customer_email = cur_customer.customer_email\n    context_view.settings = _merged_client_settings(\n        cur_customer.client_settings,\n        usr_context.sess_user.client_settings,\n    )\n    if hasattr(context_view.settings, \"ai_providers_configured\"):\n        context_view.settings.ai_providers_configured = _ai_providers_configured(cur_customer)\n    apply_2fa_client_settings(context_view.settings, usr_context.sess_user, cur_customer)\n\n    account_users_check = User.get_account_users(session=session, usr_context=usr_context)\n    if account_users_check.failure_reason != '':\n        context_view.failure_reason = account_users_check.failure_reason\n        context_view.log_id = account_users_check.log_id\n        return context_view\n\n    context_view.team_info = []\n    for account_user_row in account_users_check.user_info_list:\n        context_view.team_info.append(TeamMember(id=account_user_row.id,\n                                                 name=account_user_row.name,\n                                                 email=account_user_row.email,\n                                                 customer_admin=account_user_row.customer_admin,\n                                                 created_date=account_user_row.create_date))\n    return context_view\n\n\n@router.post(\n    \"/core/update-customer-vote-settings\",\n    summary=\"Admin: update customer-level compare/results CustomerVoteSettingsResult:\n    if not usr_context.sess_user.customer_admin:\n        log_id = log_event(\n            \"Non-admin attempted to update customer vote settings\",\n            usr_context=usr_context,\n            severity=3,\n        )\n        return CustomerVoteSettingsResult(failure_reason=\"Customer admin access required\", log_id=log_id)\n\n    customer = usr_context.sess_customer\n    if customer is None:\n        customer = Customer.get_customer_by_id_system(\n            session=session, id=usr_context.sess_user.customer_id, clear_lock=False\n        )\n    if customer is None:\n        return CustomerVoteSettingsResult(failure_reason=\"Customer not found\")\n\n    data = _parse_settings_blob(customer.client_settings)\n    # Strip retired SoftMax / triple-frame knobs\n    if form.coherence_method is not None:\n        m = str(form.coherence_method).strip().lower()\n        data[\"coherence_method\"] = m if m in (\"spearman\", \"kendall\") else \"spearman\"\n    customer.client_settings = json.dumps(data) if data else None\n    customer.update_customer_system(session=session, clear_lock=False)\n    merged = _merged_client_settings(customer.client_settings, usr_context.sess_user.client_settings)\n    apply_2fa_client_settings(merged, usr_context.sess_user, customer)\n    return CustomerVoteSettingsResult(settings=merged)\n\n\nclass CustomerAiSettingsForm(BaseModel):\n    ai_provider: str | None = Field(default=None, description=\"opencode_go, opencode_zen, or openrouter\")\n | None:\n    if not usr_context.sess_user.customer_admin:\n        log_event(\n            \"Non-admin attempted to update customer AI settings\",\n            usr_context=usr_context,\n            severity=3,\n        )\n        return \"Customer admin access required\"\n    return None\n\n\ndef _load_session_customer(session: Session, usr_context) -> Customer | None:\n    customer = usr_context.sess_customer\n    if customer is None:\n        customer = Customer.get_customer_by_id_system(\n            session=session, id=usr_context.sess_user.customer_id, clear_lock=False\n        )\n    return customer\n\n\ndef _ai_settings_view(customer: Customer) -> CustomerAiSettingsResult:\n    stored_key = customer.ai_api_key or \"\"\n    return CustomerAiSettingsResult(\n        ai_provider=customer.ai_provider or None,\n        ai_model=customer.ai_model or None,\n        ai_api_key=stored_key,\n        key_configured=bool(customer.decrypted_ai_api_key().strip()),\n        system_provider_configured=system_llm_configured(),\n        providers=list(SUPPORTED_LLM_PROVIDERS),\n    )\n\n\ndef _resolve_submitted_key(customer: Customer, submitted: str | None) -> str:\n    value = \"\" if submitted is None else str(submitted)\n    stored = customer.ai_api_key or \"\"\n    if value == stored or is_stored_secret(value):\n        return customer.decrypted_ai_api_key()\n    return value\n\n\n@router.get(\n    \"/core/customer-ai-settings\",\n    summary=\"Admin: read customer LLM provider, model, and encoded key\",\n    tags=[\"context\"],\n)\nasync def CustomerAiSettingsResult(failure_reason=denied)\n    customer = _load_session_customer(session, usr_context)\n    if customer is None:\n        return _require_customer_admin(usr_context)\n    if denied:\n        return CustomerAiSettingsResult(failure_reason=denied)\n    customer = _load_session_customer(session, usr_context)\n    if customer is None:\n        return CustomerAiSettingsResult(failure_reason=\"Customer not found\")\n    provider = str(form.ai_provider or \"\").strip()\n    if provider:\n        provider = normalize_llm_provider(provider)\n        if not provider_is_supported(provider):\n            return CustomerAiSettingsResult(failure_reason=\"Unsupported LLM provider\")\n        customer.ai_provider = provider\n    else:\n        customer.ai_provider = None\n    customer.ai_model = (str(form.ai_model or \"\").strip() or None)\n    customer.apply_ai_api_key_update(form.ai_api_key)\n    customer.update_customer_system(session=session, clear_lock=False)\n    return _ai_settings_view(customer)\n\n\n@router.post(\n    \"/core/test-customer-ai-connection\",\n    summary=\"Admin: test LLM provider credentials and list available models\",\n    tags=[\"context\"],\n)\nasync def test_customer_ai_connection(\n provider\",\n    tags=[\"context\"],\n)\nasync def llm_available_models(\n    session: SessionDep,\n    usr_context: UserAccessDep,\n) -> LlmAvailableModelsResult:\n    if not _ai_yaml_activated():\n LlmAvailableModelsResult(failure_reason=\"Customer not found\")\n    if _customer_llm_configured(customer):\n        provider = normalize_llm_provider(customer.ai_provider)\n        key = customer.decrypted_ai_api_key()\n        default_model = str(customer.ai_model or \"\").strip() or None\n        source = \"customer\"\n        default_is_system = False\n    elif \"\").strip()\n        default_model = str(getattr(s, \"AI_MODEL\", \"\") or \"\").strip() or None\n        source = \"system\"\n        default_is_system = True\n    else:\n        return LlmAvailableModelsResult(failure_reason=NO_PROVIDERS_CONFIGURED)\n    verified = verify_llm_provider(provider, key, default_model)\n    if not verified.get(\"ok\"):\n        return LlmAvailableModelsResult(\n            source=source,\n            provider=provider,\n            default_model=default_model,\n            default_is_system=default_is_system,\n   for model in (UserCustomerContextView, CustomerVoteSettingsResult):\n        model.model_fields[\"settings\"].annotation = annotation\n        model.__annotations__[\"settings\"] = annotation\n remote_ip=request.client.host)\n    user_session.create_user_session_entry(session=session)\n    response.set_cookie(\"access_token\", create_access_token(user.id), httponly=True,\n                        max_age=settings.ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES * 60,\n                        path=\"/\", samesite=\"strict\", secure=True)\n    if return_user_info:\n        return user\n    return {\"message\": auth_request(response: Response, request: Request, session: SessionDep, login: Login, return_user_info: bool=False) -> Union[UserModel, dict, Login2FAChallenge]:\n    from ..utils.login_2fa import (\n   cust = None\n\n    generic_failure_status = \"email/pw\"\n    if (user is None):\n        if not settings.is_prod():  # outside prod, we give this specific info\n            abort_code = \"email\"\n        else:\n            abort_code = generic_failure_status\n\n    if abort_code is None and not user.verify_password(session=session, password=login.password):\n        if not settings.is_prod():  # outside prod, we give this specific info\n            abort_code = \"pw\"\n        else:\n            abort_code = generic_failure_status\n\n    if abort_code is None and user.access_disabled not in (None, 0, False):\n        abort_code = \"disabled\"\n\n    if abort_code is None:\n        cust = CustomerModel.get_customer_by_id_system(session=session, id=user.customer_id)\n        if cust is None:\n            abort_code = \"customer not found\"\n\n    if abort_code is None and cust.account_status not in (AccountStatus.active,):\n        abort_code = \"customer not active\"\n\n    if abort_code is None and cust.customer_activated is False:\n        abort_code = \"customer not activated\"\n\n    if abort_code is None and cust.trial_expiration_date is not None and cust.trial_expiration_date + timedelta(days=settings.APPLICATION_ACCOUNT_TRIAL_GRACE_PERIOD_DAYS) < datetime.now():\n        abort_code = \"customer trial expired\"\n\n    if abort_code is None and cust.service_expiration_date is not None and cust.service_expiration_date + timedelta(days=settings.APPLICATION_ACCOUNT_GRACE_PERIOD_DAYS) < datetime.now():\n        abort_code = \"customer service expired\"\n\n    if abort_code is None and login_2fa_required(user, cust):\n        submitted_code = (login.two_factor_code or \"\").strip()\n        if submitted_code:\n            if verify_login_2fa_code(session, user, submitted_code):\n                return _complete_login(response, request, session, user, return_user_info)\n            abort_code = \"2fa\" if not settings.is_prod() else generic_failure_status\n        else:\n            response.set_cookie(\"access_token\", settings.CLEARED_TOKEN_VALUE, httponly=True,\n                                max_age=180,\n                                path=\"/\", samesite=\"strict\", secure=True)\n            challenge = Login2FAChallenge(\n                message=LOGIN_2FA_CHALLENGE_MESSAGE,\n                return challenge\n            code = issue_login_2fa_code(session, user)\n            email_result = await send_login_2fa_email(\n                session,\n                user=user,\n clear_login_2fa_code(session, user)\n                challenge.failure_reason = email_result\n            return challenge\n\n    if abort_code is None:\n        return _complete_login(response, change password for an email; if present, send a change pw token to the email with 1 per N minute max\",\n             tags=['auth'])\nasync def password_reset_request_wrapper(response: Response, change redundant password reset(s) ignored for {chg_user.email} - within {settings.PW_RESET_FLOODING_LIMIT_SECONDS//60} minutes', severity=2, \n                      usr_context=UserContext(sess_user=chg_user, remote_ip=request.client.host, url_path=request.url.path))\n        anti_pw_reset_flooding_times[chg_user.email][1] += 1\n        chg_user = None\n    if chg_user is not None:\n        user_pw_reset = UserPasswordReset.create_user_password_reset_system(session=session, email=chg_user.email)\n        anti_pw_reset_flooding_times[chg_user.email] = [time.time(), 1]\n        log_event(f'pw change token reset id ({user_pw_reset.id}) - {user_pw_reset.email} - request token generated', severity=1, \n                  usr_context=UserContext(sess_user=chg_user, remote_ip=request.client.host, url_path=request.url.path))\n        show_vars_semi(password_reset_request_email=chg_user.email, token=user_pw_reset.token)\n        chg_usr_context = UserContext(limited_proxy=True,\n                                      remote_ip=request.client.host, url_path=request.url.path[:150], \n                                      sess_user=chg_user)\n        chg_usr_context.sess_user.customer_admin = False   # reduce inadvertent escalation risks\n        chg_usr_context.sess_user.power_user_mode = 0\n        chg_usr_context.sess_user.system_user_mode = 0\n\n        email_result = await send_outbound_mail(session=session, usr_context=chg_usr_context, \n                                    destination_email=chg_user.email, \n                                    email_type=AuthRelatedOutboundEmailType.password_reset,\n                                    email_params=dict(user_email=chg_user.email, pw_token=user_pw_reset.token, \n on either guard (share cookie/magic-key gate instead)\n\nExemptions: routes tagged 'test-helpers' (non-production only, SessionDep-only by\ndesign) and the public /status probes.\n\"\"\"\nfrom fastapi import FastAPI\nfrom fastapi.routing import APIRoute\n\nfrom ..depends.check_api_key_dependencies import get_api_key_info\nfrom ..depends.check_user_session_jwt_dependencies import (\n get_current_user_system_admin}\nEXTERNAL_GUARDS = {get_api_key_info}\n\nEXEMPT_TAGS = {\"test-helpers\"}\nEXEMPT_PATH_SUFFIXES = (\"/status\",)\n\n\ndef _dependency_calls(route: APIRoute) -> set:\n    calls = set()\n\n    def walk(dependant) -> None:\n        calls.add(dependant.call)\n        for sub in dependant.dependencies:\n            walk(sub)\n\n    walk(route.dependant)\n    return calls\n\n\ndef _is_exempt(route: APIRoute) -> bool:\n    if EXEMPT_TAGS & set(route.tags or []):\n        return True\n    return any(route.path.endswith(suffix) for suffix in EXEMPT_PATH_SUFFIXES)\n\n\ndef verify_app_dependency_integrity(app: FastAPI, target: str) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Raise RuntimeError listing every DI violation on the given app.\"\"\"\n    violations: list[str] = []\n    for route in app.routes:\n        if not isinstance(route, APIRoute) or _is_exempt(route):\n            continue\n        calls = _dependency_calls(route)\n        methods = \",\".join(sorted(route.methods or []))\n        label = f\"{methods} {route.path} ({route.name})\"\n        if target == \"session\" and not (calls & SESSION_GUARDS):\n            violations.append(f\"{label}: missing required session-JWT dependency\")\n        elif target == \"external\" and not (calls & EXTERNAL_GUARDS):\n            violations.append(f\"{label}: missing required signed-header dependency\")\n        elif target in (\"noauth\", \"share\") and (calls & (SESSION_GUARDS | EXTERNAL_GUARDS)):\n            violations.append(f\"{label}: must not require session-JWT or signed-header dependencies\")\n    if violations:\n        raise RuntimeError(\n            f\"Dependency integrity check failed for the '{target}' application:\\n  \"\n            + \"\\n  \".join(violations)\n        )","lineStart":1,"lineEnd":70,"totalLines":70,"truncated":false}},"title":"src/pvf/utils/dependency_integrity.py","time":{"start":1787455208431,"end":1787455208781}},"metadata":{"openai":{"itemId":"fc_01a02ca1f7de7cf1800f968fd7c79201"}},"id":"prt_02ca1f9b3001wMEdJpJb1zl6tI","sessionID":"ses_fd35ea0e5ffe34F6Skn5PFxymm","messageID":"msg_02ca1efb2001sBK6Mcw6VIr1gC"}}

Field\nfrom typing import Union, Any\nfrom enum import Enum, IntEnum\nimport numpy as np\nimport secrets\n\ndef set_update_field(data_row: any, field_name: str, value: any):\n    data_row.sqlmodel_update({field_name: value})\n    return None\n\n# used for jwt token processing\nclass TokenPayload(BaseModel):\n    sub: str = None\n    exp: int = None\n\nclass TokenSchema(BaseModel):\n    access_token: str\n    refresh_token: str\n\nclass WsResultPackage(BaseModel):\n    failure_reason: str = \"\"\n    log_id: Union[int, None] = 0\n    # specific fields added via subclass\n\nclass LogSeverity(Enum):\n    debug=0\n    info=1\n    warning=2\n    error=3\n    critical=4\n    crisis=5\n    catastrophic=6\n    apocalypse=7\n    extinction_level_event=8\n\nclass AuthRelatedOutboundEmailType(Enum):\n    password_reset = 'password_reset'\n    customer_activation_self = 'customer_activation_self'\n    customer_activation_admin = 'customer_activation_admin'\n    customer_activation_admin_activated = 'customer_activation_admin_activated'\n    new_user_welcome = 'new_user_welcome'\n    magic_access_key = 'magic_access_key' # for shared link handling\n    login_2fa = 'login_2fa'\n    not_set = 'not_set'\n\nclass SubscriberTransactionType(Enum):\n    \"\"\" Note: \n    as a precaution, any transaction type containing + can only add time (pos count),\n    any containing a - can only subtract time (neg count),\n    and others must have zero toke effect\n    \"\"\"\n    purchase=\"purchase+\"\n    promotion=\"promotion+\"\n    credit=\"credit+\"\n    usage=\"usage-\"\n    cancelled=\"cancelled-\"\n    note=\"note\"\n    balance_forward=\"balance_forward\"\n\nclass SubscriberTransactionUnits(Enum):\n    days = 'days'\n    months = 'months'\n    years = 'years'\n\nclass PvfClientAuthSettings(BaseModel):\n    TWO_FACTOR_AUTH_ENABLED: bool = False\n    TWO_FACTOR_AUTH_OPTIONAL: bool = False\n    TWO_FACTOR_AUTH_CUSTOMER_CONTROLLABLE: bool = False\n    enable_user_communication: bool = False\n    ai_features_enabled: bool = False\n    ai_providers_configured: bool = False\n\nbase_32_chars = \"23456789ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ\"\n\ndef get_random_baseN_value(length=6, map_chars=base_32_chars, *, digits_only=False):\n    if digits_only:\n        secret_string = ''.join(secrets.choice('0123456789') for _ in range(length))\n    elif map_chars:\n        secret_string = ''.join(secrets.choice(map_chars) for _ in range(length))\n    else:\n        secret_string = secrets.token_urlsafe(length)[:length]\n    return secret_string\n","lineStart":1,"lineEnd":84,"totalLines":84,"truncated":false}},"title":"src/pvf/utils/pvf_base_internal_resources.py","time":{"start":1787455213669,"end":1787455214140}},"metadata":{"openai":{"itemId":"fc_01a02ca20cf07642b9158645b0a0ffbe"}},"id":"prt_02ca20d75001djg7uDBJ59uIst","sessionID":"ses_fd35ea0e5ffe34F6Skn5PFxymm","messageID":"msg_02ca2007f001Kh62hIcDYmCl23"}}

{"type":"tool_use","timestamp":1787455216933,"sessionID":"ses_fd35ea0e5ffe34F6Skn5PFxymm","part":{"type":"tool","tool":"read","callID":"call_01a02ca219ef7331b3c3f4d4225085df","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"filePath":"/workspaces/powerchoice/README.md"},"output":"<path>/workspaces/powerchoice/README.md</path>\n<type>file</type>\n<content>\n1: # Power Choice Pro\n2: \n3: Structured decision support: teams define a **Project**, add **Options** and **Factors**, **Compare** them pairwise, and review **Results**. Share links collect participant input (Compare Only / Compare and See Only Your Results / See Full Results). User-facing language lives in [`docs_examples/terminology_dictionary.md`](docs_examples/terminology_dictionary.md).\n4: \n5: Python 3.13 + FastAPI + SQLModel + Alembic (Postgres). Nuxt 3 SPA in `client/` generates into `static_client/` (gitignored) and is served by FastAPI.\n6: \n7: ## Pratt Ventures Framework (pvf) — keep isolated\n8: \n9: The app runs on **pvf** (`src/pvf/`). pvf is a reusable multi-tenant kernel (session JWT, customer/user admin, email, share links, signed external APIs, Stripe, branding, logging). It may be managed or replaced as a separate tree. **Keep it isolated.**\n10: \n11: Current isolation patterns (do not erode these):\n12: \n13: - **pvf never imports this application.** The runner loads the shell named in `src/pvf_app_startup.yaml` (`src.app_shell` / `app_startup`). Application code reaches pvf only through published entry points — see [`src/pvf/README.md`](src/pvf/README.md).\n14: - **Do not put Power Choice logic inside `src/pvf/`.** Product routes, models, ranking, and UI bindings belong in `src/api/`, `src/db/`, `src/utils/`, `src/app_shell.py`, and `client/`.\n15: - **Do not reach past published pvf surfaces** (`pvf_app_runner`, `pvf_get_alembic_config`, `pvf.utils.pvf_services`, `pvf.pvf_invocation`, `pvf.config.pvf_config_settings`, `pvf.depends.*`, `pvf.db.models.*`).\n16: - **Extend, don’t fork.** Extra columns on pvf tables go through `register_schema_extensions` in the shell. Feature switches and share vocabularies stay in `src/pvf_app_startup.yaml` (deployment-fixed, read-only at runtime).\n17: - Framework tables live in `src/pvf/db/models/`; application tables live in `src/db/models/` and are registered from `src/db/models/bootstrap.py`.\n18: \n19: ## Directory structure\n20: \n21: | Path | Purpose |\n22: |------|---------|\n23: | `src/` | Backend. Poetry project (`package-mode = false`); in-project venv is `src/.venv`. |\n24: | `src/pvf/` | **Framework only.** Runner, four FastAPI apps, auth/share/external/Stripe/email, framework models. Treat as a separate product. |\n25: | `src/pvf_app_startup.yaml` | Deployment-fixed pvf config: shell module, env file name, feature switches, share types. |\n26: | `src/app_shell.py` | Application bridge: settings merge, hooks, routers, schema extensions. Shared with the watcher. |\n27: | `src/api/` | Power Choice session/share/external routes (projects, options/factors, compare, results). |\n28: | `src/db/models/` | Application tables (projects, options/factors, vote events). Wired in `bootstrap.py`. |\n29: | `src/utils/` | Application services (ranking, compare session, templates, user context). |\n30: | `src/config/` | Application `GlobalSettings` (extends pvf settings) and client-facing settings. |\n31: | `src/alembic/` | Migrations. `env.py` is a thin shim over `pvf_get_alembic_config`. |\n32: | `src/powerchoice_watcher.py` | Separate process: signed webhook callback delivery. Not started by `./start-server.sh`. |\n33: | `src/powerchoice_server.py` | Compatibility shim that re-exports the pvf runner `app`. |\n34: | `src/tests/` | API tests against the configured DB (not hermetic). Helpers in `src/tests/helpers/`. |\n35: | `client/` | Nuxt 3 SPA (`ssr: false`) + Vue 3 + Vuetify. Dev on `:3000`, proxies API to `:8100`. |\n36: | `static_client/` | Generated SPA FastAPI serves. Gitignored. Rebuild with `./scripts/nuxt/update_static_client.sh`. |\n37: | `static/` | Non-SPA static files (`/static`). |\n38: | `scripts/` | Migrations, OpenAPI extract, Nuxt publish, Stripe CLI helpers. |\n39: | `e2e/` | Playwright. Starts/reuses API `:8100` + Nuxt `:3000`. |\n40: | `email_templates/` | SMTP-Jinja templates (html/txt/subject). |\n41: | `docs_examples/` | Product language, share-mode notes, prototypes — not runtime. |\n42: | `build/` | Docker image, supervisord, sample compose. |\n43: | `.devcontainer/` | Dev container + Postgres 16 sidecar. |\n44: | `openapi_sessions.json` / `openapi_api.json` | Contract snapshots for the session UI surface and customer toolkit API. |\n45: | `example.env` | Runtime env template. Copy to `.env` at the repo root (`*.env` is gitignored). |\n46: | `project_templates.yml` | Seed project / option / factor templates. |\n47: | `AGENTS.md` | Engineering conventions for this repo (commands, testing, OpenAPI). |\n48: \n49: ## HTTP surfaces\n50: \n51: Composed by `src/pvf/pvf_app_runner.py`, then handed to `src/app_shell.py`:\n52: \n53: | App | Mount | Auth | Role |\n54: |-----|-------|------|------|\n55: | root | `/` | — | Static + SPA fallback + Stripe hooks; `/status`, `/alive` |\n56: | noauth | `/auth-ws` | none | Login, password reset, self-registration |\n57: | session | `/ws` | JWT cookie | Session UI API |\n58: | share | `/ext-ws` | share cookie / magic key | Share-link compare and results |\n59: | external | `/api` | signed headers | Customer toolkit API |\n60: \n61: Session OpenAPI: `/session/docs`. Toolkit OpenAPI: `/api/docs`.\n62: \n63: ## Local development\n64: \n65: Dev container (recommended): reopen in the container, then:\n66: \n67: ```bash\n68: mkdir -p /workspaces/powerchoice/postgres-data-${USER}   # before first compose start\n69: cp example.env .env                                       # set UNIQUE_CONFIGURATION_CHECK=1 and secrets\n70: cd src && poetry install\n71: cd ..\n72: ./scripts/migrations/run-migrations.sh\n73: ./start-server.sh                                         # fastapi dev → 0.0.0.0:8100\n74: ```\n75: \n76: UI in another terminal:\n77: \n78: ```bash\n79: cd client && npm install && npm run dev                   # :3000, proxies to :8100\n80: ```\n81: \n82: Ship the SPA with the API: `./scripts/nuxt/update_static_client.sh` (required after any `client/` change).\n83: \n84: Default DSN (compose sidecar): `postgresql+psycopg://powerchoice:powerchoice_dev_password@postgres:5432/powerchoice`.\n85: \n86: ```bash\n87: # Tests (hit the real configured DB)\n88: src/.venv/bin/pytest src/tests/\n89: \n90: # Browser e2e\n91: cd e2e && npm install && npx playwright install chromium && npm test\n92: ```\n93: \n94: More commands: migrations, OpenAPI refresh, version bump — see [`AGENTS.md`](AGENTS.md).\n95: \n96: ## Git remote\n97: \n98: This repo’s `origin` is its own remote (e.g. `git@gh-powerchoice:Pratt-Ventures/power-choice.git`). There is no local bare sibling. If `git ls-remote origin` fails with “dubious ownership”, add `git config --global --add safe.directory /workspaces/powerchoice`.\n\n(End of file - total 98 lines)\n</content>","metadata":{"preview":"# Power Choice Pro\n\nStructured decision support: teams define a **Project**, add **Options** and **Factors**, **Compare** them pairwise, and review **Results**. A client side lightweight middleware approach can be used to perform the injection. \\n\\nTwo fields have been added to config_settings, ANALYTIC_TRACKING_TOKEN, and ANALYTIC_TRACKING_STATIC_SCRIPT. The first is a specific token value that binds the site and instance (e.g., PowerChoice Pro and dev, staging, or production) to the analytic service.  The configuration parameters are copied on startup to the client_config, so they are available in the client_context and in server logic. \\n\\nThe client context is only available after login and not in the same form for shared sessions. from __future__ import annotations\n2: \n3: from datetime import datetime\n4: \n5: from fastapi import APIRouter, File, Form, HTTPException, Request, UploadFile, status\n6: from fastapi.responses import Response\n7: from pydantic import BaseModel\n8: \n9: from ..db.models.branding_images import (\n10:     BrandingImageResult,\n11:     CustomerBrandingImage,\n12:     ProjectBrandingImage,\n13: )\n14: from ..db.models.customer_user import Customer\n15: from ..db.models.share_link_tracking import ShareLink\n16: from ..depends.api_session_dependencies import SessionDep\n17: from ..depends.check_user_session_jwt_dependencies import UserAccessDep\n18: from ..bindings.pvf_invocation import get_hooks\n19: from ..utils.pvf_base_internal_resources import WsResultPackage\n20: from ..utils.branding_image import process_branding_upload\n21: from ..utils.log_event import log_event\n22: from .share_link_manage import get_token_cookie, make_token_cookie\n23: \n24: router = APIRouter()\n25: share_router = APIRouter()\n26: \n27: \n28: class BrandingMeta(BaseModel):\n29:     customer_name: str | None = None\n30:     has_customer_image: bool = False\n31:     customer_image_modify_date: datetime | None = None\n32:     project_title: str | None = None\n33:     has_project_image: bool = False\n34:     project_image_modify_date: datetime | None = None\n35: \n36: \n37: class ShareBrandingMetaResult(WsResultPackage):\n38:     branding: BrandingMeta | None = None\n39: \n40: \n41: def _row_meta(row) -> BrandingImageResult:\n42:     if row is None:\n43:         return BrandingImageResult(has_image=False)\n44:     return BrandingImageResult(\n45:         has_image=True,\n46:         content_type=row.content_type,\n47:         file_name=row.file_name,\n48:         original_file_name=getattr(row, \"original_file_name\", None) or row.file_name,\n49:         original_byte_size=(\n50:             getattr(row, \"original_byte_size\", None)\n51:             if getattr(row, \"original_byte_size\", None) is not None\n52:             else row.byte_size\n53:         ),\n54:         byte_size=row.byte_size,\n55:         was_compressed=bool(getattr(row, \"was_compressed\", False)),\n56:         modify_date=row.modify_date,\n57:     )\n58: \n59: \n60: async def _read_processed_upload(file: UploadFile):\n61:     data = await file.read()\n62:     try:\n63:         return process_branding_upload(\n64:             data,\n65:             content_type_hint=file.content_type,\n66:             filename=file.filename,\n67:         )\n68:     except ValueError as exc:\n69:         raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=str(exc)) from exc\n70: \n71: \n72: def _meta_from_rows(\n73:     *,\n74:     customer: Customer | None,\n75:     project_display_name: str | None,\n76:     cust_img: CustomerBrandingImage | None,\n77:     proj_img: ProjectBrandingImage | None,\n78: ) -> BrandingMeta:\n79:     return BrandingMeta(\n80:         customer_name=customer.customer_name if customer else None,\n81:         has_customer_image=cust_img is not None,\n82:         customer_image_modify_date=cust_img.modify_date if cust_img else None,\n83:         project_title=project_display_name,\n84:         has_project_image=proj_img is not None,\n85:         project_image_modify_date=proj_img.modify_date if proj_img else None,\n86:     )\n87: \n88: \n89: def _resolve_entity_for_user(session, usr_context, entity_id: int):\n90:     \"\"\"Resolve and tenancy-check an application entity via the registered entity_resolver hook.\n91: \n92:     Returns the PvfResolvedEntity, or None when the entity does not exist or belongs to\n93:     a different customer.\n94:     \"\"\"\n95:     resolver = get_hooks().entity_resolver\n96:     if resolver is None:\n97:         log_event(\"Branding image store requires the application entity_resolver hook\", severity=4,\n98:                   raise_exception=HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=\"Branding image support is not fully configured\"))\n99:     resolved = resolver(session=session, usr_context=usr_context, entity_id=entity_id)\n100:     if resolved is None or resolved.customer_id != usr_context.sess_user.customer_id:\n101:         return None\n102:     return resolved\n103: \n104: \n105: def _resolve_share_link(session, token: str):\n106:     result = ShareLink.get_shared_link_by_id_or_magic_token_system(\n107:         session=session,\n108:         shared_magic_token=token,\n109:         clear_lock=False,\n110:         allow_disabled_link_retrieval=False,\n111:     )\n112:     if result.failure_reason or result.link_info is None:\n113:         return None, result.failure_reason or \"Share link not found\"\n114:     return result.link_info, None\n115: \n116: \n117: def _share_session_authenticated(request: Request, shared_magic_token: str) -> bool:\n118:     \"\"\"True when the httponly share view cookie is present and valid for this token.\"\"\"\n119:     view_token, display_name, email, make_cookies_better = get_token_cookie(request)\n120:     if view_token is None or make_cookies_better is None:\n121:         return False\n122:     expected, _ = make_token_cookie(\n123:         shared_magic_token=shared_magic_token,\n124:         display_name=display_name,\n125:         email=email,\n126:         make_cookies_better=make_cookies_better,\n127:     )\n128:     return view_token == expected\n129: \n130: \n131: def _require_share_session(request: Request, token: str) -> None:\n132:     if not _share_session_authenticated(request, token):\n133:         raise HTTPException(\n134:             status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,\n135:             detail=\"Share authentication required to download branding images\",\n136:         )\n137: \n138: \n139: def _image_response(row) -> Response:\n140:     return Response(\n141:         content=bytes(row.image_data),\n142:         media_type=row.content_type,\n143:         headers={\"Cache-Control\": \"private, max-age=300\"},\n144:     )\n145: \n146: \n147: def _upsert_kwargs(processed) -> dict:\n148:     return dict(\n149:         content_type=processed.content_type,\n150:         image_data=processed.image_data,\n151:         file_name=processed.file_name,\n152:         original_file_name=processed.original_file_name,\n153:         original_byte_size=processed.original_byte_size,\n154:         was_compressed=processed.was_compressed,\n155:     )\n156: \n157: \n158: # ---- Authenticated workspace branding ----\n159: \n160: \n161: @router.get(\n162:     \"/branding/customer-meta\",\n163:     summary=\"Workspace branding image metadata\",\n164:     tags=[\"context\"],\n165: )\n166: async def get_customer_branding_meta(\n167:     session: SessionDep, usr_context: UserAccessDep\n168: ) -> BrandingImageResult:\n169:     customer_id = usr_context.sess_user.customer_id\n170:     row = CustomerBrandingImage.get_by_customer_id_system(\n171:         session=session, customer_id=customer_id, clear_lock=False\n172:     )\n173:     return _row_meta(row)\n174: \n175: \n176: @router.get(\n177:     \"/branding/customer-image\",\n178:     summary=\"Download workspace branding image\",\n179:     tags=[\"context\"],\n180: )\n181: async def get_customer_branding_image(session: SessionDep, usr_context: UserAccessDep):\n182:     customer_id = usr_context.sess_user.customer_id\n183:     row = CustomerBrandingImage.get_by_customer_id_system(\n184:         session=session, customer_id=customer_id, clear_lock=False\n185:     )\n186:     if row is None:\n187:         raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=\"No branding image\")\n188:     return _image_response(row)\n189: \n190: \n191: @router.post(\n192:     \"/branding/customer-image\",\n193:     summary=\"Admin: upload or replace workspace branding image\",\n194:     tags=[\"context\"],\n195: )\n196: async def upload_customer_branding_image(\n197:     session: SessionDep,\n198:     usr_context: UserAccessDep,\n199:     file: UploadFile = File(...),\n200: ) -> BrandingImageResult:\n201:     if not usr_context.sess_user.customer_admin:\n202:         log_id = log_event(\n203:             \"Non-admin attempted to upload customer branding image\",\n204:             usr_context=usr_context,\n205:             severity=3,\n206:         )\n207:         return BrandingImageResult(failure_reason=\"Customer admin access required\", log_id=log_id)\n208:     processed = await _read_processed_upload(file)\n209:     row = CustomerBrandingImage.upsert_system(\n210:         session=session,\n211:         customer_id=usr_context.sess_user.customer_id,\n212:         clear_lock=False,\n213:         **_upsert_kwargs(processed),\n214:     )\n215:     return _row_meta(row)\n216: \n217: \n218: @router.delete(\n219:     \"/branding/customer-image\",\n220:     summary=\"Admin: delete workspace branding image\",\n221:     tags=[\"context\"],\n222: )\n223: async def delete_customer_branding_image(\n224:     session: SessionDep, usr_context: UserAccessDep\n225: ) -> BrandingImageResult:\n226:     if not usr_context.sess_user.customer_admin:\n227:         log_id = log_event(\n228:             \"Non-admin attempted to delete customer branding image\",\n229:             usr_context=usr_context,\n230:             severity=3,\n231:         )\n232:         return BrandingImageResult(failure_reason=\"Customer admin access required\", log_id=log_id)\n233:     CustomerBrandingImage.delete_by_customer_id_system(\n234:         session=session, customer_id=usr_context.sess_user.customer_id, clear_lock=False\n235:     )\n236:     return BrandingImageResult(has_image=False)\n237: \n238: \n239: @router.get(\n240:     \"/branding/project-meta\",\n241:     summary=\"Project branding image metadata\",\n242:     tags=[\"manage-projects\"],\n243: )\n244: async def get_project_branding_meta(\n245:     session: SessionDep,\n246:     usr_context: UserAccessDep,\n247:     project_id: int,\n248: ) -> import BaseModel\n\nfrom ..db.models.branding_images import (\n    BrandingImageResult,\n    CustomerBrandingImage,\n    ProjectBrandingImage,\n)\nfrom ..db.models.customer_user import Customer\nfrom ..db.models.share_link_tracking import ShareLink\nfrom ..depends.api_session_dependencies import SessionDep\nfrom ..depends.check_user_session_jwt_dependencies import UserAccessDep\nfrom ..bindings.pvf_invocation import get_hooks\nfrom ..utils.pvf_base_internal_resources import WsResultPackage\nfrom ..utils.branding_image import process_branding_upload\nfrom ..utils.log_event import log_event\nfrom .share_link_manage import get_token_cookie, make_token_cookie\n\nrouter = APIRouter()\nshare_router = APIRouter()\n\n\nclass BrandingMeta(BaseModel):\n    customer_name: str | None = None\n    has_customer_image: bool = False\n    customer_image_modify_date: datetime | None = None\n    project_title: str | None = None\n    has_project_image: bool = False\n    project_image_modify_date: datetime | None = None\n\n\nclass ShareBrandingMetaResult(WsResultPackage):\n    branding: BrandingMeta | None = None\n\n\ndef _row_meta(row) -> BrandingImageResult:\n    if row is None:\n        return BrandingImageResult(has_image=False)\n    return BrandingImageResult(\n        has_image=True,\n        content_type=row.content_type,\n        file_name=row.file_name,\n        original_file_name=getattr(row, \"original_file_name\", None) or row.file_name,\n        original_byte_size=(\n            getattr(row, \"original_byte_size\", None)\n            if getattr(row, \"original_byte_size\", None) is not None\n            else row.byte_size\n        ),\n        byte_size=row.byte_size,\n        was_compressed=bool(getattr(row, \"was_compressed\", False)),\n        modify_date=row.modify_date,\n    )\n\n\nasync def _read_processed_upload(file: UploadFile):\n    data = await file.read()\n    try:\n        return process_branding_upload(\n            data,\n            content_type_hint=file.content_type,\n            filename=file.filename,\n        )\n    except ValueError as exc:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=str(exc)) from exc\n\n\ndef _meta_from_rows(\n    *,\n    customer: Customer | None,\n    project_display_name: str | None,\n    cust_img: CustomerBrandingImage | None,\n    proj_img: ProjectBrandingImage | None,\n) -> BrandingMeta:\n    return BrandingMeta(\n        customer_name=customer.customer_name if customer else None,\n        has_customer_image=cust_img is not None,\n        customer_image_modify_date=cust_img.modify_date if cust_img else None,\n        project_title=project_display_name,\n        has_project_image=proj_img is not None,\n        project_image_modify_date=proj_img.modify_date if proj_img else None,\n    )\n\n\ndef _resolve_entity_for_user(session, usr_context, entity_id: int):\n    \"\"\"Resolve and tenancy-check an application entity via the registered entity_resolver hook.\n\n    Returns the PvfResolvedEntity, or None when the entity does not exist or belongs to\n    a different customer.\n    \"\"\"\n    resolver = get_hooks().entity_resolver\n    if resolver is None:\n        log_event(\"Branding image store requires the application entity_resolver hook\", severity=4,\n                  raise_exception=HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=\"Branding image support is not fully configured\"))\n    resolved = resolver(session=session, usr_context=usr_context, entity_id=entity_id)\n    if resolved is None or resolved.customer_id != usr_context.sess_user.customer_id:\n        return None\n    return resolved\n\n\ndef _resolve_share_link(session, token: str):\n    result = ShareLink.get_shared_link_by_id_or_magic_token_system(\n        session=session,\n        shared_magic_token=token,\n        clear_lock=False,\n        allow_disabled_link_retrieval=False,\n    )\n    if result.failure_reason or result.link_info is None:\n        return None, result.failure_reason or \"Share link not found\"\n    return result.link_info, None\n\n\ndef _share_session_authenticated(request: Request, shared_magic_token: str) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"True when the httponly share view cookie is present and valid for this token.\"\"\"\n    view_token, display_name, email, make_cookies_better = get_token_cookie(request)\n    if view_token is None or make_cookies_better is None:\n        return False\n    expected, _ = make_token_cookie(\n        shared_magic_token=shared_magic_token,\n        display_name=display_name,\n        email=email,\n        make_cookies_better=make_cookies_better,\n    )\n    return view_token == expected\n\n\ndef _require_share_session(request: Request, token: str) -> None:\n    if not _share_session_authenticated(request, token):\n        raise HTTPException(\n            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,\n            detail=\"Share authentication required to download branding images\",\n        )\n\n\ndef _image_response(row) -> Response:\n    return Response(\n        content=bytes(row.image_data),\n        media_type=row.content_type,\n        headers={\"Cache-Control\": \"private, max-age=300\"},\n    )\n\n\ndef _upsert_kwargs(processed) -> dict:\n    return dict(\n        content_type=processed.content_type,\n        image_data=processed.image_data,\n        file_name=processed.file_name,\n        original_file_name=processed.original_file_name,\n        original_byte_size=processed.original_byte_size,\n        was_compressed=processed.was_compressed,\n    )\n\n\n# ---- Authenticated workspace session=session, customer_id=customer_id, clear_lock=False\n    )\n    return _row_meta(row)\n\n\n@router.get(\n    \"/branding/customer-image\",\n    summary=\"Download workspace branding image\",\n    tags=[\"context\"],\n)\nasync def get_customer_branding_image(session: SessionDep, usr_context: UserAccessDep):\n    customer_id = usr_context.sess_user.customer_id\n    row = CustomerBrandingImage.get_by_customer_id_system(\n        session=session, customer_id=customer_id, clear_lock=False\n    )\n    if row is None:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=\"No branding image\")\n    return _image_response(row)\n\n\n@router.post(\n    \"/branding/customer-image\",\n    summary=\"Admin: upload or replace workspace branding image\",\n    tags=[\"context\"],\n)\nasync def upload_customer_branding_image(\n    session: SessionDep,\n    usr_context: UserAccessDep,\n    file: UploadFile = File(...),\n) -> BrandingImageResult:\n    if not severity=3,\n        )\n        return BrandingImageResult(failure_reason=\"Customer admin access required\", log_id=log_id)\n    processed = await _read_processed_upload(file)\n    row = CustomerBrandingImage.upsert_system(\n        session=session,\n        customer_id=usr_context.sess_user.customer_id,\n        clear_lock=False,\n        **_upsert_kwargs(processed),\n    )\n    return _row_meta(row)\n\n\n@router.delete(\n    \"/branding/customer-image\",\n    summary=\"Admin: delete workspace branding image\",\n    tags=[\"context\"],\n)\nasync def return BrandingImageResult(has_image=False)\n\n\n@router.get(\n    \"/branding/project-meta\",\n    summary=\"Project branding image metadata\",\n    tags=[\"manage-projects\"],\n)\nasync def get_project_branding_meta(\n    session: SessionDep,\n    usr_context: UserAccessDep,\n    project_id: int,\n) -> BrandingImageResult:\n    customer_id = usr_context.sess_user.customer_id\n    if ProjectBrandingImage.get_by_project_id_system(\n        session=session, customer_id=customer_id, project_id=project_id, clear_lock=False\n    )\n    return _row_meta(row)\n\n\n@router.get(\n clear_lock=False\n    )\n    if row is None:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=\"No branding image\")\n    return _image_response(row)\n\n\n@router.post(\n Access Url; Enter Name Only; Password required from share; Token email sent (based on share value); Use link or key to verify; Access Granted.\n33:     ANALYTIC_TRACKING_TOKEN: str = settings.ANALYTIC_TRACKING_TOKEN\n34:     ANALYTIC_TRACKING_STATIC_SCRIPT: str = settings.ANALYTIC_TRACKING_STATIC_SCRIPT\n35: \n36: \n37:     @field_validator(\"coherence_method\")\n38:     @classmethod\n39:     def _clamp_coherence(cls, v: str) -> str:\n40:         m = (v or \"spearman\").strip().lower()\n41:         return m if m in (\"spearman\", \"kendall\") else \"spearman\"\n42: \n43: client_settings = ClientSettings()\n\n(End of file - total 43 lines)\n</content>","metadata":{"preview":"from pydantic import Field, field_validator\nfrom pydantic_settings import BaseSettings, SettingsConfigDict\nfrom os import environ, getpid\nfrom socket import gethostname\n\nfrom .config_settings import settings\nfrom ..pvf.bindings.pvf_invocation import PvfClientAuthSettings\n\nclass ClientSettings(PvfClientAuthSettings, BaseSettings):\n    process_name: str = Field(default=settings.process_name, description=\"the computer from which the service response was generated\")\n    environment_name: str = Field(default=settings.environment_name, description=\"the attributed environment name from which the service response was generated\")\n    computer_name: str = Field(default=settings.computer_name, description=\"the computer from which the service response was generated\")\n    process_id: int = Field(default=settings.process_id, description=\"the computer process id from which the service response was generated\")\n    server_env: str = Field(default=settings.server_env, description=\"Server environment settings for the computer generating the response.\")\n    coherence_method: str = Field(\n        default=\"spearman\",\n        description=\"Rank agreement algorithm for multi-participant reports: spearman | kendall.\",\n    )\n    # each of these enables the respective ShareAccessCheckMode value in the user interface for share links.\n    # all are implemented in the backend and front end flows, but options may be reduced for simplicity here.","truncated":false,"loaded":[],"display":{"type":"file","path":"/workspaces/powerchoice/src/config/config_client.py","text":"from pydantic import Field, field_validator\nfrom pydantic_settings import BaseSettings, SettingsConfigDict\nfrom os import environ, getpid\nfrom socket import gethostname\n\nfrom .config_settings import settings\nfrom ..pvf.bindings.pvf_invocation import PvfClientAuthSettings\n\nclass ClientSettings(PvfClientAuthSettings, BaseSettings):\n    process_name: str = Field(default=settings.process_name, description=\"the computer from which the service response was generated\")\n    environment_name: str = Field(default=settings.environment_name, description=\"the attributed environment name from which the service response was generated\")\n    computer_name: str = Field(default=settings.computer_name, description=\"the computer from which the service response was generated\")\n    process_id: int = Field(default=settings.process_id, description=\"the computer process id from which the service response was generated\")\n    server_env: str = Field(default=settings.server_env, description=\"Server environment settings for the computer generating the response.\")\n    coherence_method: str = Field(\n        default=\"spearman\",\n        description=\"Rank agreement algorithm for multi-participant reports: spearman | kendall.\",\n    )\n    # each of these enables the respective ShareAccessCheckMode value in the user interface for share links.\n    # all are implemented in the backend and front end flows, but options may be reduced for simplicity here.\n    enable_share_open_access : bool = settings.ENABLE_SHARE_OPEN_ACCESS   # Access Url; Enter Name; Access granted.\n    enable_share_email_any_unverified : bool = settings.ENABLE_SHARE_EMAIL_ANY_UNVERIFIED   # Access Url; Enter Name & Any Email (not verified); Access Granted.\n    enable_share_email_any_verified : bool = settings.ENABLE_SHARE_EMAIL_ANY_VERIFIED       # Access Url; Enter Name & Any Email; Token email sent; Follow link or enter key from token email; Access Granted.\n    enable_share_email_matching : bool = settings.ENABLE_SHARE_EMAIL_MATCHING               # Access Url; Enter Name & Email; Email must match share; Access Granted.\n    enable_share_email_matching_verified : bool = settings.ENABLE_SHARE_EMAIL_MATCHING_VERIFIED   # Access Url; Enter Name & Email; Email must match share; Token email sent; Use link or key to verify; Access Granted.\n    enable_share_recipient_email_verified : bool = settings.ENABLE_SHARE_RECIPIENT_EMAIL_VERIFIED # Access Url; Enter Name Only; Token email sent (based on share value); Use link or key to verify; Access Granted.\n    enable_share_password_only : bool = settings.ENABLE_SHARE_PASSWORD_ONLY                        # Access Url; Enter Name Only; Password required from share; Access Granted.\n    enable_share_password_with_email_any_unverified : bool = settings.ENABLE_SHARE_PASSWORD_WITH_EMAIL_ANY_UNVERIFIED   # Access Url; Enter Name & Any Email (not verified); Password required from share; Access Granted.\n    enable_share_password_with_email_any_verified : bool = settings.ENABLE_SHARE_PASSWORD_WITH_EMAIL_ANY_VERIFIED       # Access Url; Enter Name & Any Email (verified); Password required from share; Token email sent; Use link or key to verify; Access Granted.\n    enable_share_password_with_email_matching : bool = settings.ENABLE_SHARE_PASSWORD_WITH_EMAIL_MATCHING          # Access Url; Enter Name & Email; Email must match share; Password required from share; Access Granted.\n    enable_share_password_with_email_matching_verified : bool = settings.ENABLE_SHARE_PASSWORD_WITH_EMAIL_MATCHING_VERIFIED   # Access Url; Enter Name & Email; Email must match share; Password required from share; Token email sent; Use link or key to verify; Access Granted.\n    enable_share_password_with_recipient_email_verified : bool = settings.ENABLE_SHARE_PASSWORD_WITH_RECIPIENT_EMAIL_VERIFIED # Access Url; Enter Name Only; Password required from share; Token email sent (based on share value); Use link or key to verify; Access Granted.\n    ANALYTIC_TRACKING_TOKEN: str = settings.ANALYTIC_TRACKING_TOKEN\n    ANALYTIC_TRACKING_STATIC_SCRIPT: str = settings.ANALYTIC_TRACKING_STATIC_SCRIPT\n\n\n    @field_validator(\"coherence_method\")\n    @classmethod\n    def _clamp_coherence(cls, v: str) -> str:\n        m = (v or \"spearman\").strip().lower()\n        return m if m in (\"spearman\", \"kendall\") else \"spearman\"\n\nclient_settings = ClientSettings()","lineStart":1,"lineEnd":43,"totalLines":43,"truncated":false}},"title":"src/config/config_client.py","time":{"start":1787455374781,"end":1787455374897}},"metadata":{"openai":{"itemId":"fc_01a02ca483da7c40a46385ee78a07548"}},"id":"prt_02ca485b00010jCVOM4hZFz3lQ","sessionID":"ses_fd35ea0e5ffe34F6Skn5PFxymm","messageID":"msg_02ca47f00001pLALr7zW2MEMUi"}}
{"type":"step_finish","timestamp":1787455375241,"sessionID":"ses_fd35ea0e5ffe34F6Skn5PFxymm","part":{"id":"prt_02ca4874d001g10RzZa7wwk8zF","reason":"tool-calls","snapshot":"a6a1c13372fb2d6d750979985eaa7409f9ffb88c","messageID":"msg_02ca47f00001pLALr7zW2MEMUi","sessionID":"ses_fd35ea0e5ffe34F6Skn5PFxymm","type":"step-finish","tokens":{"total":96826,"input":148,"output":53,"reasoning":0,"cache":{"write":0,"read":96625}},"cost":0.00021865}}
