{"type":"tool_use","timestamp":1787455385367,"sessionID":"ses_fd35ea0e5ffe34F6Skn5PFxymm","part":{"type":"tool","tool":"write","callID":"call_01a02ca48f2c7780a13b46351d9d3b98","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"filePath":"/workspaces/powerchoice/src/utils/analytic_tracking.py","content":"\"\"\"Analytic tracking payload resolver.\n\nDerives the effective script string from GlobalSettings / ClientSettings\nwith guard conditions and whitelist suffix matching.\n\nRules (strict, per plan Q2/Q3):\n- Both ANALYTIC_TRACKING_TOKEN and ANALYTIC_TRACKING_STATIC_SCRIPT must be non-empty/whitespace, otherwise no injection.\n- Empty whitelist means none enabled (suppress).\n- Single entry '*' means allow all domains.\n- Otherwise every absolute URL (src/href) in the effective string must have a host suffix match against the whitelist (case-insensitive, dot-boundary for domain suffix, port-aware when entry contains ':').\n- Token substitution replaces all occurrences of \"{ANALYTIC_TRACKING_TOKEN}\".\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\nfrom urllib.parse import urlparse\n\n_PLACEHOLDER = \"{ANALYTIC_TRACKING_TOKEN}\"\n_SRC_HREF_RE = re.compile(r'\\b(?:src|href)\\s*=\\s*[\"\\']([^\"\\']+)[\"\\']', re.IGNORECASE)\n\n\ndef _normalize_whitelist(raw) -> list[str]:\n    if raw is None:\n        return []\n    if isinstance(raw, str):\n        # CSV string\n        return [s.strip() for s in raw.split(\",\") if s.strip()]\n    if isinstance(raw, (list, tuple, set)):\n        return [str(s).strip() for s in raw if str(s).strip()]\n    return []\n\n\ndef _extract_urls(script: str) -> list[str]:\n    return _SRC_HREF_RE.findall(script or \"\")\n\n\ndef _is_whitelisted_url(url: str, whitelist: list[str]) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Suffix match per spec.\n\n    - Relative URLs (no host/netloc) are allowed (same-origin).\n    - Entry with ':' is matched against netloc (host:port), otherwise against hostname.\n    - Matching is case-insensitive, suffix with dot-boundary to avoid \"notrybbit.io\" matching \"rybbit.io\".\n    - '*' already handled at caller.\n    \"\"\"\n    try:\n        parsed = urlparse(url)\n        hostname = (parsed.hostname or \"\").lower()\n        netloc = (parsed.netloc or \"\").lower()\n        # Relative URL: no host, no netloc -> allow\n        if not hostname and not netloc:\n            # urlparse may still have path only; treat as relative -> allow\n            if not parsed.scheme:\n                return True\n            return True\n        for entry in whitelist:\n            e = entry.strip().lower()\n            if e == \"*\":\n                return True\n            if not e:\n                continue\n            if \":\" in e:\n                cand = netloc\n                # Exact or suffix with dot\n                if cand == e or cand.endswith(\".\" + e) or cand.endswith(e):\n                    # For port-bearing entries, require dot-boundary or exact. Use strict dot for domain part.\n                    # Allow cand == e or cand.endswith(\".\" + e)\n                    if cand == e or cand.endswith(\".\" + e):\n                        return True\n                    # Fallback: raw suffix (handles edge where entry includes dot+port)\n                    if cand.endswith(e):\n                        # ensure preceding char is '.' or ':' to avoid partial match\n                        idx = cand.rfind(e)\n                        if idx > 0 and cand[idx - 1] in (\".\", \":\"):\n                            return True\n                        if idx == 0:\n                            return True\n            else:\n                # match against hostname\n                if not hostname:\n                    continue\n                if hostname == e or hostname.endswith(\".\" + e):\n                    return True\n        return False\n    except Exception:\n        return False\n\n\ndef get_effective_tracking_script() -> str | None:\n    \"\"\"Return effective payload string or None when injection should be suppressed.\n\n    Reads from GlobalSettings (src.config.config_settings.settings) which is the\n    source of truth after merge_pvf_settings_into; client_config mirrors it but\n    resolver uses settings directly for unauthenticated endpoint (no client context).\n    \"\"\"\n    from ..config.config_settings import settings\n\n    token = (getattr(settings, \"ANALYTIC_TRACKING_TOKEN\", \"\") or \"\").strip()\n    script = (getattr(settings, \"ANALYTIC_TRACKING_STATIC_SCRIPT\", \"\") or \"\").strip()\n    whitelist_raw = getattr(settings, \"ANALYTIC_TRACKING_WHITELIST\", [])\n\n    whitelist = _normalize_whitelist(whitelist_raw)\n\n    # Guard: requires both populated (strict)\n    if not token:\n        return None\n    if not script:\n        return None\n\n    # Empty whitelist => none enabled\n    if not whitelist:\n        try:\n            from ..pvf.utils.log_event import log_event\n\n            log_event(\"analytic tracking suppressed: whitelist empty (none enabled)\", severity=1)\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n        return None\n\n    # Substitute all placeholder occurrences\n    effective = script.replace(_PLACEHOLDER, token)\n\n    # Whitelist: '*' allows all\n    if len(whitelist) == 1 and whitelist[0].strip() == \"*\":\n        return effective\n\n    urls = _extract_urls(effective)\n    for url in urls:\n        if not _is_whitelisted_url(url, whitelist):\n            try:\n                from ..pvf.utils.log_event import log_event\n\n                log_event(\n                    f\"analytic tracking suppressed: URL {url!r} not in whitelist {whitelist}\",\n                    severity=2,\n                )\n            except Exception:\n                pass\n            return None\n\n    return effective\n"},"output":"Wrote file successfully.","metadata":{"diagnostics":{},"filepath":"/workspaces/powerchoice/src/utils/analytic_tracking.py","exists":false,"truncated":false},"title":"src/utils/analytic_tracking.py","time":{"start":1787455385301,"end":1787455385358}},"metadata":{"openai":{"itemId":"fc_01a02ca48f2c7780a13b46351d9d3b98"}},"id":"prt_02ca48f65001e7fNxp7Ly2wKDL","sessionID":"ses_fd35ea0e5ffe34F6Skn5PFxymm","messageID":"msg_02ca488ad001SItpeshrKwo1VH"}}

{"type":"tool_use","timestamp":1787455391196,"sessionID":"ses_fd35ea0e5ffe34F6Skn5PFxymm","part":{"type":"tool","tool":"write","callID":"call_01a02ca4b68b718390b0085e1347e2b1","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"filePath":"/workspaces/powerchoice/src/api/analytic_tracking.py","content":"\"\"\"Unauthenticated analytic tracking payload endpoint.\n\nGET /api/_public/analytic-tracking  (mounted on /api, exempt from signed-header DI)\nand also alias on noauth for completeness: GET /_public/analytic-tracking\n\nNo authentication, no session, no DB. Returns:\n- 200 text/html; charset=utf-8 with effective script string when enabled\n- 204 No Content when suppressed (empty token/script, whitelist block, etc.)\n\nCache-Control: no-store, private — client owns sessionStorage caching.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter\nfrom fastapi.responses import PlainTextResponse, Response\n\nfrom ..utils.analytic_tracking import get_effective_tracking_script\n\n# Router intended for external app at /api (path relative => /api/_public/...)\nexternal_router = APIRouter()\n\n# Alias router for noauth app at /auth-ws (path relative => /auth-ws/_public/...)\nnoauth_router = APIRouter()\n\n\ndef _build_response() -> Response:\n    payload = get_effective_tracking_script()\n    if payload is None or not payload.strip():\n        # 204 distinguishes \"off\" from error; no body\n        return Response(status_code=204, headers={\"Cache-Control\": \"no-store, private\"})\n    return PlainTextResponse(\n        content=payload,\n        media_type=\"text/html; charset=utf-8\",\n        headers={\"Cache-Control\": \"no-store, private\"},\n    )\n\n\n@external_router.get(\n    \"/_public/analytic-tracking\",\n    summary=\"Unauthenticated analytic tracking script payload\",\n    description=\"Returns the effective <script> string after token substitution and whitelist check, or 204 when disabled. No authentication required; works pre-login and for shared sessions. Restart required for config changes.\",\n    tags=[\"public\"],\n    response_class=PlainTextResponse,\n)\nasync def get_analytic_tracking_external():\n    return _build_response()\n\n\n@noauth_router.get(\n    \"/_public/analytic-tracking\",\n    summary=\"Unauthenticated analytic tracking script payload (noauth alias)\",\n    description=\"Alias for /api/_public/analytic-tracking on the no-auth app. Same payload/contract.\",\n    tags=[\"public\"],\n    response_class=PlainTextResponse,\n)\nasync def get_analytic_tracking_noauth():\n    return _build_response()\n"},"output":"Wrote file successfully.","metadata":{"diagnostics":{},"filepath":"/workspaces/powerchoice/src/api/analytic_tracking.py","exists":false,"truncated":false},"title":"src/api/analytic_tracking.py","time":{"start":1787455391148,"end":1787455391187}},"metadata":{"openai":{"itemId":"fc_01a02ca4b68b718390b0085e1347e2b1"}},"id":"prt_02ca4b7b2001YBqAZ3nIk1bdRB","sessionID":"ses_fd35ea0e5ffe34F6Skn5PFxymm","messageID":"msg_02ca4b13c001DY0kDHmBKF2ng3"}}

(","truncated":false,"loaded":[],"display":{"type":"file","path":"/workspaces/powerchoice/src/pvf/utils/dependency_integrity.py","text":"\"\"\"Boot-time dependency-injection integrity verification for the composed FastAPI apps.\n\nDependency injection is strictly enforced here, regardless of whether the dependency\ndata is an input parameter for a given endpoint: the runner refuses to start if any\nroute is missing the guard its application requires (or carries a guard that would\nbreak its access model).\n\n  - session app:  every route must depend on the session-JWT guard (or an admin variant)\n  - external app: every route must depend on the signed-header guard\n  - noauth app:   no route may depend on either guard (no authorization expected)\n  - share app:    no route may depend on either guard (share cookie/magic-key gate instead)\n\nExemptions: routes tagged 'test-helpers' (non-production only, SessionDep-only by\ndesign) and the public /status probes.\n\"\"\"\nfrom fastapi import FastAPI\nfrom fastapi.routing import APIRoute\n\nfrom ..depends.check_api_key_dependencies import get_api_key_info\nfrom ..depends.check_user_session_jwt_dependencies import (\n    get_current_user,\n    get_current_user_customer_admin,\n    get_current_user_system_admin,\n)\n\nSESSION_GUARDS = {get_current_user, get_current_user_customer_admin, get_current_user_system_admin}\nEXTERNAL_GUARDS = {get_api_key_info}\n\nEXEMPT_TAGS = {\"test-helpers\"}\nEXEMPT_PATH_SUFFIXES = (\"/status\",)\n\n\ndef _dependency_calls(route: APIRoute) -> set:\n    calls = set()\n\n    def walk(dependant) -> None:\n        calls.add(dependant.call)\n        for sub in dependant.dependencies:\n            walk(sub)\n\n    walk(route.dependant)\n    return calls\n\n\ndef _is_exempt(route: APIRoute) -> bool:\n    if EXEMPT_TAGS & set(route.tags or []):\n        return True\n    return any(route.path.endswith(suffix) for suffix in EXEMPT_PATH_SUFFIXES)\n\n\ndef verify_app_dependency_integrity(app: FastAPI, target: str) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Raise RuntimeError listing every DI violation on the given app.\"\"\"\n    violations: list[str] = []\n    for route in app.routes:\n        if not isinstance(route, APIRoute) or _is_exempt(route):\n            continue\n        calls = _dependency_calls(route)\n        methods = \",\".join(sorted(route.methods or []))\n        label = f\"{methods} {route.path} ({route.name})\"\n        if target == \"session\" and not (calls & SESSION_GUARDS):\n            violations.append(f\"{label}: missing required session-JWT dependency\")\n        elif target == \"external\" and not (calls & EXTERNAL_GUARDS):\n            violations.append(f\"{label}: missing required signed-header dependency\")\n        elif target in (\"noauth\", \"share\") and (calls & (SESSION_GUARDS | EXTERNAL_GUARDS)):\n            violations.append(f\"{label}: must not require session-JWT or signed-header dependencies\")\n    if violations:\n        raise RuntimeError(\n            f\"Dependency integrity check failed for the '{target}' application:\\n  \"\n            + \"\\n  \".join(violations)\n        )","lineStart":1,"lineEnd":70,"totalLines":70,"truncated":false}},"title":"src/pvf/utils/dependency_integrity.py","time":{"start":1787455397513,"end":1787455397950}},"metadata":{"openai":{"itemId":"fc_01a02ca4ddb37f40a8259656d30670ac"}},"id":"prt_02ca4ddee001UhPSnR5s6jCuq1","sessionID":"ses_fd35ea0e5ffe34F6Skn5PFxymm","messageID":"msg_02ca4c8dc0017pZWzm7B34fmKK"}}

{"type":"tool_use","timestamp":1787455434652,"sessionID":"ses_fd35ea0e5ffe34F6Skn5PFxymm","part":{"type":"tool","tool":"write","callID":"call_01a02ca54a547420956753244276d3b4","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"filePath":"/workspaces/powerchoice/client/plugins/analytic-tracking.client.ts","content":"/**\n * Analytic Tracking injection plugin — lightweight client-side middleware.\n *\n * Fetches effective payload once per browser session (sessionStorage) from\n * unauthenticated endpoint GET /api/_public/analytic-tracking (primary) and\n * injects it as the last child of <head> on every page render.\n *\n * Contract:\n * - 204 or empty => no injection (sentinel __EMPTY__ cached to avoid refetch)\n * - 200 text/html with <script>...</script> => substituted, whitelisted payload\n * - Idempotent: single element id=\"analytic-tracking-script\", re-appended if head replaced\n * - Timing: after document.head exists, on initial load + SPA navigations + MutationObserver fallback\n */\n\nconst STORAGE_KEY = 'analyticTracking:payload:v1'\nconst SENTINEL_EMPTY = '__EMPTY__'\nconst SCRIPT_ID = 'analytic-tracking-script'\nconst ENDPOINT_PRIMARY = '/api/_public/analytic-tracking'\nconst ENDPOINT_FALLBACK = '/auth-ws/_public/analytic-tracking'\n\nlet memoryCache: string | null = null\nlet fetchPromise: Promise<string | null> | null = null\n\nfunction safeGetStorage(): string | null {\n  try {\n    if (typeof window === 'undefined' || !window.sessionStorage) return memoryCache\n    return window.sessionStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY)\n  } catch {\n    return memoryCache\n  }\n}\n\nfunction safeSetStorage(value: string): void {\n  try {\n    if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && window.sessionStorage) {\n      window.sessionStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, value)\n    } else {\n      memoryCache = value\n    }\n  } catch {\n    memoryCache = value\n  }\n  if (memoryCache !== null && value !== SENTINEL_EMPTY) {\n    // keep memory in sync when sessionStorage available\n    memoryCache = value\n  } else if (value === SENTINEL_EMPTY) {\n    memoryCache = SENTINEL_EMPTY\n  }\n}\n\nfunction getCachedPayload(): string | null {\n  const raw = safeGetStorage()\n  if (raw === null) return null\n  if (raw === SENTINEL_EMPTY) return SENTINEL_EMPTY\n  return raw\n}\n\nfunction injectHeadScript(payload: string | null): void {\n  if (!payload || payload === SENTINEL_EMPTY || !payload.trim()) return\n  if (typeof document === 'undefined' || !document.head) return\n\n  // Idempotency: if already correctly placed, skip\n  const existing = document.getElementById(SCRIPT_ID)\n  if (existing) {\n    const isLast = document.head.lastElementChild === existing\n    const isConnected = existing.isConnected && existing.parentElement === document.head\n    if (isConnected && isLast) return\n    // Otherwise remove to re-append as last child\n    existing.remove()\n  }\n\n  const trimmed = payload.trim()\n  if (!trimmed) return\n\n  // Parse using temporary container\n  const temp = document.createElement('div')\n  temp.innerHTML = trimmed\n  const scriptNodes = temp.querySelectorAll('script')\n\n  if (scriptNodes.length === 0) {\n    // Fallback: if payload is not wrapped in <script>, treat whole payload as script string?\n    // Create a script element with payload as text? But spec says payload is <SCRIPT> tag, so no-op.\n    return\n  }\n\n  scriptNodes.forEach((node, idx) => {\n    const s = document.createElement('script')\n    // Copy all attributes from source node\n    for (const attr of Array.from(node.attributes)) {\n      s.setAttribute(attr.name, attr.value)\n    }\n    // Only first script gets the sentinel id (ensures exactly one tracked element)\n    if (idx === 0) s.id = SCRIPT_ID\n    // Ensure empty contents handling: copy textContent (should be empty per spec)\n    s.textContent = node.textContent || ''\n    // If multiple scripts, append in order; last appended will be last child of head\n    document.head.appendChild(s)\n  })\n}\n\nasync function fetchPayload(): Promise<string | null> {\n  const tryFetch = async (url: string): Promise<Response> => {\n    return fetch(url, {\n      method: 'GET',\n      credentials: 'omit',\n      headers: { Accept: 'text/html, text/plain, */*' },\n    })\n  }\n\n  try {\n    let response = await tryFetch(ENDPOINT_PRIMARY)\n    // Fallback to alias if primary 404 (e.g., mount mismatch)\n    if (response.status === 404) {\n      try {\n        response = await tryFetch(ENDPOINT_FALLBACK)\n      } catch {\n        // keep primary response\n      }\n    }\n\n    if (response.status === 204) {\n      safeSetStorage(SENTINEL_EMPTY)\n      return SENTINEL_EMPTY\n    }\n    if (!response.ok) {\n      safeSetStorage(SENTINEL_EMPTY)\n      return SENTINEL_EMPTY\n    }\n    const text = (await response.text()).trim()\n    if (!text) {\n      safeSetStorage(SENTINEL_EMPTY)\n      return SENTINEL_EMPTY\n    }\n    safeSetStorage(text)\n    return text\n  } catch {\n    safeSetStorage(SENTINEL_EMPTY)\n    return SENTINEL_EMPTY\n  }\n}\n\nasync function ensurePayloadAndInject(): Promise<void> {\n  const cached = getCachedPayload()\n  if (cached !== null) {\n    if (cached !== SENTINEL_EMPTY) injectHeadScript(cached)\n    return\n  }\n\n  if (!fetchPromise) {\n    fetchPromise = fetchPayload()\n  }\n  const payload = await fetchPromise\n  if (payload && payload !== SENTINEL_EMPTY) {\n    // Ensure head exists before injecting; if not yet, wait for DOMContentLoaded\n    if (typeof document !== 'undefined' && document.head) {\n      injectHeadScript(payload)\n    } else if (typeof document !== 'undefined') {\n      document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => injectHeadScript(payload), { once: true })\n    }\n  }\n}\n\nfunction scheduleInjectFromCache(): void {\n  const cached = getCachedPayload()\n  if (cached && cached !== SENTINEL_EMPTY) {\n    if (typeof document !== 'undefined' && document.head) {\n      injectHeadScript(cached)\n    } else if (typeof document !== 'undefined') {\n      document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {\n        const c = getCachedPayload()\n        if (c && c !== SENTINEL_EMPTY) injectHeadScript(c)\n      }, { once: true })\n    }\n  }\n}\n\nexport default defineNuxtPlugin(() => {\n  if (import.meta.server) return\n\n  // Initial fetch/inject after head is available\n  if (typeof document !== 'undefined') {\n    if (document.readyState === 'loading') {\n      document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {\n        void ensurePayloadAndInject()\n      }, { once: true })\n    } else {\n      void ensurePayloadAndInject()\n    }\n\n    // Also attempt immediate if cached and head already exists (covers SPA hydration)\n    scheduleInjectFromCache()\n  }\n\n  // SPA navigation: re-apply on route change (idempotent)\n  try {\n    const router = useRouter()\n    router.afterEach(() => {\n      scheduleInjectFromCache()\n      // If cache miss (rare, first navigation before fetch resolved), trigger fetch\n      const cached = getCachedPayload()\n      if (cached === null) void ensurePayloadAndInject()\n    })\n  } catch {\n    // useRouter not available in some contexts\n  }\n\n  // MutationObserver fallbacks: handle head replacement or late head mutations\n  if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && typeof MutationObserver !== 'undefined') {\n    const observeHead = () => {\n      if (!document.head) return\n      const headObserver = new MutationObserver(() => {\n        const cached = getCachedPayload()\n        if (cached && cached !== SENTINEL_EMPTY) {\n          const existing = document.getElementById(SCRIPT_ID)\n          const needInject = !existing || existing.parentElement !== document.head || document.head.lastElementChild !== existing\n          if (needInject) injectHeadScript(cached)\n        }\n      })\n      headObserver.observe(document.head, { childList: true })\n\n      // Observe documentElement for head replacement (e.g., full head swap)\n      const docObserver = new MutationObserver((mutations) => {\n        for (const m of mutations) {\n          for (const node of Array.from(m.addedNodes)) {\n            if ((node as Element).tagName === 'HEAD') {\n              const cached = getCachedPayload()\n              if (cached && cached !== SENTINEL_EMPTY) injectHeadScript(cached)\n              // re-attach head observer to new head\n              try { headObserver.disconnect() } catch {}\n              if (document.head) headObserver.observe(document.head, { childList: true })\n            }\n          }\n        }\n      })\n      if (document.documentElement) {\n        docObserver.observe(document.documentElement, { childList: true })\n      }\n    }\n\n    if (document.head) {\n      observeHead()\n -d node_modules ]; then\n  echo \"Installing client dependencies...\"\n  npm install\nfi\n\n# Empty apiBase → relative same-origin requests when UI is served by FastAPI\necho \"Generating static client (NUXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE=)...\"\nNUXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE= npm run generate\n\nif [ ! -f \"$OUTPUT_PUBLIC/index.html\" ]; then\n  echo \"error: generate did not produce $OUTPUT_PUBLIC/index.html\" >&2\n  exit 1\nfi\n\n# Guard: production SPA HTML must link entry CSS (missing = unstyled UI)\nif ! grep -q 'rel=\"stylesheet\".*entry\\..*\\.css\\|entry\\..*\\.css.*rel=\"stylesheet\"\\|/_nuxt/entry\\.' \"$OUTPUT_PUBLIC/index.html\"; then\n  if ! grep -q 'entry\\.[^\"]*\\.css' \"$OUTPUT_PUBLIC/index.html\"; then\n    echo \"error: generated index.html has no entry CSS link; check nuxt SPA build config\" >&2\n    echo \"  (the vite:configResolved entry→server hack must not run during generate)\" >&2\n    exit 1\n  fi\nfi\n\necho \"Replacing $STATIC_CLIENT_DIR ...\"\nrm -rf \"$STATIC_CLIENT_DIR\"\nmkdir -p \"$STATIC_CLIENT_DIR\"\ncp -a \"$OUTPUT_PUBLIC\"/. \"$STATIC_CLIENT_DIR\"/\n\necho \"Done.
